    &#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;</span></p>
    <p class="Tho" align="center" style="text-indent: 0cm; line-height: normal; margin-top: 6.0pt; background-image:url('none'); background-repeat:repeat; background-attachment:scroll">
    <span style="font-size: 20pt; color: #00FF00; text-transform: uppercase"><b>
    <span style="font-family: Tahoma">NH&#7882; &#272;&#7896; MAI</span></b></span></p>
    <p class="Bodytext" align="center" style="text-indent: 0cm; background: #333300">
    <b>
    <span style="font-size: 14.0pt; font-family: Times New Roman; color: #FFFF99">
    * </span></b>
    <span style="font-size: 14pt; color: #FFFF99; background-position: 0 0"><b>
    <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khuy&#7871;t danh</span></b></span></p>
    <p align="center">
    <span lang="HE" dir="RTL" style="font-size: 12.0pt; font-family: Times New Roman; color: #FF6600; layout-grid-mode: line">
    &#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;&#8362;</span></td>
  </tr>

<p class="MsoNormal" align="center"><b>
<span style="color:red">&nbsp;</span></b></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-weight: 700; font-family: Times New Roman">I</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      &nbsp;</p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoá nhi th&#259;m th&#7859;m nghìn trùng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7855;c c&acirc;n phúc t&#7897;i, rút vòng 
      v&#7847;n xoay.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngàn x&#432;a m&#7845;y k&#7867; gian ngay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xem c&#417; báo &#7913;ng bi&#7871;t tay tr&#7901;i 
      già.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tu&#7847;n hoàn l&#7869; &#7845;y ch&#7859;ng xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7899; &#273;em n&ocirc;ng n&#7895;i mà ng&#7901; cao 
      xanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i nào ph&#7909; k&#7867; trung trinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Dù v&#432;&#417;ng n&#7841;n &#7845;y, &#7855;t dành phúc 
      kia.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Danh th&#417;m mu&ocirc;n ki&#7871;p còn ghi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7875; g&#432;&#417;ng trong sách, t&#7841;c bia 
      d&#432;&#7899;i &#273;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gian tà &#273;&#7855;c chí m&#7845;y h&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7855;t th&#7847;n kh&ocirc;n gi&#7845;u, l&#432;&#7899;i tr&#7901;i 
      kh&ocirc;n dung. </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Uy quy&#7873;n m&#7897;t phút nh&#432; kh&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xem b&#7857;ng l&#7917;a &#273;á, ví cùng &#273;ám 
      m&acirc;y.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thanh nhàn khi t&#7921;a hiên t&acirc;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vui lòng &#273;èn sách ngh&#7881; tay v&#259;n 
      bài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7879;n ngoài xem «Nh&#7883; &#273;&#7897; mai»,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nhà &#272;&#432;&#7901;ng truy&#7873;n v&#7883; &#273;&#7871;n &#273;&#7901;i 
      &#272;&#7913;c T&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;&#7901;ng-ch&acirc;u có k&#7867; thanh trung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bá Cao là ch&#7919;, v&#7889;n dòng h&#7885; Mai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;i&#7873;m hùng s&#7899;m &#273;&atilde; sinh trai
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i cho v&#259;n t&#432;&#7899;ng kén tài 
      tr&#7841;ng nguyên </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7863;t cho L&#432;&#417;ng Ng&#7885;c là tên
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tài hoa &#273;áng b&#7921;c trích tiên 
      d&#432;&#7899;i &#273;&#7901;i </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#417; kia &#273;&atilde; ph&#7843;i duyên tr&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&ocirc;n nh&acirc;n &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7883;nh v&#7873; n&#417;i h&#7885; 
      H&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7881; vì ngo&#7841;i l&#7925; b&#7845;y l&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#432;a trao l&#7877; nh&#7841;n, m&#7899;i &#273;&#7847;u 
      thi&#7871;p canh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng tri huy&#7879;n L&#7883;ch-thành,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">G&#432;&#417;ng trung sáng v&#7863;c, túi thanh 
      nh&#7865; b&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7847;m &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng ngày tháng thung 
      dung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tên ngay là ti&#7871;t, n&#432;&#7899;c trong 
      &#7845;y lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7845;y l&acirc;u L&#432; K&#7927; t&#432;&#7899;ng c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tuy quy&#7873;n t&#432;&#7899;c l&#7899;n, mà phong 
      &#273;&#7897; hèn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Túi tham c&#7911;a &#273;út ch&#7853;t lèn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Dung bên gian &#273;&#7843;ng, ghét bên 
      hi&#7873;n tài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng m&#7845;y &#273;&#7897; quan ngoài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7841;n bè nh&#7901; có m&#7845;y ng&#432;&#7901;i &#273;&#7891;ng 
      niên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong tri&#7873;u h&#7871;t s&#7913;c gi&#7919; gìn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; ngôi thiêm s&#7921;, ng&#432;&#7901;i quy&#7873;n 
      th&#432;&#7907;ng th&#432;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kìa &#272;&#7843;ng Ti&#7871;n, n&#7885; &#272;&ocirc;ng S&#417;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; toà &#272;&ocirc; sát, ng&#432;&#7901;i toà Hàn 
      l&acirc;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chu toàn trong ngo&#7841;i m&#432;&#7901;i 
      n&#259;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bè trung ch&#7889;n v&#7919;ng, m&#432;u th&acirc;m ch&#7899; hòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t ngày huy&#7879;n v&#7909; v&#7915;a xong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vào trong &ocirc;ng m&#7899;i ng&#7887; cùng phu 
      nh&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7857;ng: «Ta v&#7889;n k&#7867; trung th&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Trên vì n&#432;&#7899;c, d&#432;&#7899;i vì dân m&#7899;i 
      là!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ví b&#7857;ng theo thói ng&#432;&#7901;i ta,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«U&#7889;n l&#432;ng co g&#7889;i c&#361;ng nh&#417; m&#7897;t 
      &#273;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#7885; là c&#7847;u c&#7841;nh chi ai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7859;ng trong lang mi&#7871;u c&#361;ng 
      ngoài &#273;i&#7873;n viên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Con ta tr&#7841;c tu&#7893;i thanh niên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Có g&#432;&#417;ng khoa giáp, có n&#7873;n 
      &#273;&#7881;nh chung.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Sao cho gi&#7919; &#273;&#432;&#7907;c ch&#7919; trung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7899;i là hi&#7871;u t&#7917; n&#7889;i dòng th&#432; 
      h&#432;&#417;ng.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7919;ng là vui truy&#7879;n n&#7897;i &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7843;nh ngoài b&#7895;ng có hai chàng 
      báo tin:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng ph&#7909;ng ch&#7881; th&#259;ng 
      thuyên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chi&#7871;u tr&#7901;i mây v&#7895;, ân trên m&#432;a 
      nhu&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tri&#7873;u ban, d&#7921; b&#7853;c quan th&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i khoa c&#7845;p s&#7921; gi&#7919; ph&#7847;n gián 
      quan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tin &#273;&acirc;u khêu t&#7845;m trung can,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t hai quy&#7871;t h&#7859;n tr&#7915; gian 
      phen này.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n làm ti&#7879;c r&#432;&#7907;u vui v&#7847;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7863;n dò gia s&#7921; &#273;&#7883;nh ngày kh&#7903;i 
      th&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tàng tàng chén cúc ba tu&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;òi c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; v&#7899;i phu nh&acirc;n d&#7841;y 
      l&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7857;ng: «B&#7845;y l&acirc;u nh&#7919;ng &#7903; ngoài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«D&#7841; này t&#7845;m t&#7913;c v&#7899;i ng&#432;&#7901;i quy&#7873;n 
      gian.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Rày vâng &#273;ài gián th&#259;ng quan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Phen này ta quy&#7871;t c&#7843; gan phen

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&#7907;p nhau nào bi&#7871;t có ngày n&#7919;a

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&#7845;y gi&#7901; phu quí, ph&#7909; vinh,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;òi công t&#7917; l&#7841;i m&#7865; con bàn

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngoài &#273;&#7847;y v&#417;i chén, trong mê 
      m&#7849;n tình.</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n r&#7857;ng: «Bu&#7893;i lên kinh,

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ph&#7887;ng cho m&#7845;y &#273;&#7913;a tu&#7923; hành 
      h&#7847;u &ocirc;ng?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «L&#7885; chi &#273;&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7897;t chàng Mai B&#7841;ch v&#7889;n trong 
      h&#7885; nhà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«G&#7885;i là làm b&#7841;n &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«G&#7885;i là ngày gió ngày m&#432;a &#273;i 
      h&#7847;u.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Còn &#273;&#432;&#417;ng trò chuy&#7879;n tr&#432;&#7899;c 
      sau, </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">X&ocirc;n xao nghe b&#7895;ng ti&#7871;ng &#273;&acirc;u

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hang men móc v&#432;&#7907;n, c&acirc;y xào x&#7841;c

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      &#273;&#7895; c&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kìa chài s&#7899;m, n&#7885; cày tr&#432;a,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gió g&#7847;n gi&#7885;ng m&#7909;c, m&acirc;y xa 
      ti&#7871;ng ti&#7873;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quê ng&#432;&#7901;i phong c&#7843;nh &#273;ìu hiu,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#259;ng thanh, gió mát d&#432;&#7901;ng 
      chi&#7873;u chu&#7897;ng ai!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7919;ng màng dong ru&#7893;i d&#7863;m dài,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng ra b&#7895;ng th&#7845;y b&#7889;n ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      xa xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7887;i ra m&#7899;i bi&#7871;t r&#7857;ng là:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong kinh, l&#7841;i b&#7897; sai nha &#273;ón 
      mình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoàng h&ocirc;n gác bóng chênh 
      chênh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n tìm quán khách t&#7841;m 
      &#273;ình ngh&#7881; ng&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7889;n ng&#432;&#7901;i t&#7913;c kh&#7855;c v&acirc;ng l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c &#273;i tìm ch&#7889;n th&#7843;nh th&#417;i 
      &#273;ón m&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tuy r&#7857;ng quán khách h&#7865;p hòi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7889;n n&#7857;m c&#361;ng t&#297;nh, ch&#7889;n ng&#7891;i 
      c&#361;ng thanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thung dung h&#7887;i chuy&#7879;n trong 
      kinh:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#432;, Hoàng l&#361; &#7845;y tung hoành ra 
      sao?</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;a r&#7857;ng: «Ch&#7913;c tr&#7885;ng quy&#7873;n 
      cao,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tri&#7873;u quan quá n&#7917;a ra vào làm 
      t&ocirc;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ai ai khoá mi&#7879;ng b&#7883;t h&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ngang vua phú quí, nghiêng 
      tr&#7901;i uy linh.</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7841;i gia rày &#7903; quan kinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Theo &#273;òi nhi&#7873;u ít th&#7871; tình là 
      xong.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng n&#7893;i gi&#7853;n &#273;ùng &#273;ùng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7857;ng: «Phen này quy&#7871;t ch&#7859;ng 
      dung loài h&#7891;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Vào &#273;&acirc;y ta s&#7869; hay cho,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7915;ng Tung, &#273;&#7915;ng K&#7927;, h&#7871;t L&#432;, 
      h&#7871;t Hoàng!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7863;t nào b&#7855;t ch&#432;&#7899;c th&#7871; th&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Thi&#7871;t tha ban t&#7889;i, khoe 
      khoang gi&#7919;a ngày.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gan càng t&#7913;c, ru&#7897;t càng &#273;&#7847;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n th&ocirc;i cu&#7897;c r&#432;&#7907;u, vào

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sáng mai th&#7913;c d&#7853;y tr&ocirc;ng quanh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Treo trên th&#7845;y có b&#7913;c tranh 
      Di, T&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#432; khêu t&#7845;m d&#7841; trung nghì,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7841;y &#273;em nghiên bút tay &#273;&#7873; b&#7889;n 
      c&acirc;u .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Than r&#7857;ng: «Thanh &#7913;ng khí 
      c&#7847;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432;&#7901;i kim c&#7893;, d&#7841; tr&#432;&#7899;c sau 
      m&#7897;t &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i truy&#7873;n gi&#7909;c ki&#7879;u lên

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng kinh qu&#7889;c &#273;&atilde; t&#7845;c gang &#273;ó

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t vùng riêng &#273;&#7863;t ph&#7911; tr&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoàng thành ch&#259;m ch&#7855;m, k&#7923; &#273;ài 
      cao cao.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&otilde;i ng&#432;&#7901;i n&#432;&#7899;c Nh&#432;&#7907;c, ngu&#7891;n

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Li&#7873;n m&acirc;y ngàn d&atilde;y, bày sao 
      tr&#259;m toà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7853;p ch&#7891;ng vách g&#7845;m, t&#432;&#7901;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7917;a l&#7847;u ng&#361; ph&#432;&#7907;ng, th&#7873;m nhà 
      l&#432;&#7905;ng long.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bách qu&acirc;n &#273;óng ch&#7863;t s&acirc;n phong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7871;ng chen xe ng&#7921;a, v&#7867; l&#7891;ng 
      c&acirc;n &#273;ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tìm vào L&#7841;i b&#7897; t&#7899;i n&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n nha m&ocirc;n, &#273;&#7883;nh ngày mai 
      ti&#7871;n ch&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7855;m xiêm, s&#7917;a m&#361; gi&#7901; l&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">U&#7889;n l&#432;ng n&#259;m l&#7841;y, kh&#7845;u &#273;&#7847;u ba

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;y r&#7891;i ren rén t&acirc;u lên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chúc c&acirc;u v&#7841;n tu&#7871;, d&acirc;ng trên 
      c&#7917;u trùng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7871;ng tr&#7901;i &#273;&#432;a l&#7841;i b&#7879; r&#7891;ng:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7863;t toà gián vi&#7879;n«, kén dùng 
      k&#7867; trung.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Sao cho s&#7855;t &#273;á m&#7897;t lòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Mi&#7879;ng hùm ch&#7899; s&#7907;, v&#7849;y r&#7891;ng 
      ch&#7899; ghê.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&acirc;u r&#7857;ng: «H&#7893; ph&#7853;n ty vi
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;em lòng khuy&#7875;n mã &#273;&#7873;n nghì 
      cao sâu.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7873;m &#273;an v&#7915;a bu&#7893;i tan trào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bách quan làm l&#7877; kh&#7845;u &#273;&#7847;u lui 
      ra,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng toan tr&#7903; l&#7841;i nhà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7879;n g&#7847;n t&#432;&#7899;ng ph&#7911; sang qua 
      vào li&#7873;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghiêm trang c&#7917;a th&#7871; s&acirc;n 
      quy&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c bia h&#7841; m&atilde; ch&#7853;t lèn ng&#7921;a 
      xe.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng bèn gi&#7843; cách v&ocirc; tri,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7891;i ch&#259;m ch&#7855;m ki&#7879;u, vào k&#7873; 
      nghi m&ocirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&ocirc;n quan tr&ocirc;ng th&#7845;y thét d&#7891;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">X&ocirc;n xao b&#7867; bót, &#7891;n &#7891;n h&#7887;i tra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «&#272;&acirc;u ch&#7859;ng bi&#7871;t ta,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Huy&#7879;n quan v&#7873; b&#7897; l&#7841;i khoa

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Bu&#7893;i ch&#7847;u nh&acirc;n ti&#7879;n sang &#273;&acirc;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nh&#7901; ai trong &#7845;y b&#7849;m thay cho 
      t&#432;&#7901;ng.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&ocirc;n quan r&#7857;ng: «L&#7879; ph&#7911; &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Có &#273;&#7891; y&#7871;t l&#7877;, có vàng m&#7845;y 
      bao.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Có thì s&#7869; b&#7849;m cho vào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&#7857;ng kh&ocirc;ng h&atilde;y ch&#7921;c ngoài rào 
      xa xa.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «L&#7879; &#273;&#7863;t bao gi&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#7844;y là qu&#7889;c pháp hay là ph&#7911; 
      quy?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ta &#273;&acirc;y v&#7889;n ch&#7859;ng c&#7847;n chi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Vào thì c&#361;ng &#273;&#432;&#7907;c, ra thì c&#361;ng 
      nên!</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&ocirc;ng xu&#7889;ng ki&#7879;u, v&#7851;n ng&#7891;i

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tay c&#7847;m th&#7911; b&#7843;n ném bên th&#7873;m 
      ngoài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kíp truy&#7873;n qu&acirc;n ki&#7879;u tháo lui,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sang toà &#273;&ocirc; sát, h&#7885;p ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      &#273;&#7891;ng niên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phùng c&ocirc;ng ra &#273;ón r&#432;&#7899;c li&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7911; t&acirc;n m&#7897;t h&#7897;i, hàn huyên m&#7845;y 
      l&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7921; tình k&#7875; l&#7875; l&ocirc;i th&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngoài mu&ocirc;n ngàn d&#7863;m, trong 
      m&#432;&#7901;i m&#7845;y n&#259;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xi&#7871;t bao trò chuy&#7879;n tri &acirc;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khói h&#432;&#417;ng cao th&#7845;p, chén tr&#7847;m 
      &#273;&#7847;y v&#417;i</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «Lúc m&#7899;i r&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7913;c gan mà l&#7841;i n&#7921;c c&#432;&#7901;i l&#7855;m 
      sao.</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7875; t&#7915; t&#432;&#7899;ng ph&#7911; m&#7899;i vào,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7871; nào khinh d&#7875;, th&#7871; nào h&#7887;i 
      han.</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mi&#7879;ng &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng l&#7841;i, m&#7863;t m&ocirc;n quan,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; xin &#273;òi l&#7877;, mình toan ném

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Mu&#7889;n yên chi kh&#7887;i l&#7845;y &#273;&#7847;u h&#7885;

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Rày l&#7847;n mai l&#7919;a, nh&#7853;p nhù khó

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7875; mà xem k&#7927; v&#7899;i Mai th&#7871; nào!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ví b&#7857;ng gi&#7843;i k&#7871;t làm sao,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Gi&#7919; gìn Mai th&#7883; g&#7905; vào l&#7889;i

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Già này dù thác c&#361;ng vinh,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Su&#7889;i vàng khu&#7845;t m&#7863;t c&#361;ng

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tho&#7855;t th&ocirc;i t&#7915; t&#7841; m&#7845;y ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7845;c lòng th&#7873; ch&#7859;ng &#273;&#7897;i tr&#7901;i

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngày xu&acirc;n th&#7845;m tho&#7855;t &#273;&#432;a thoi,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432; c&ocirc;ng tu&#7893;i th&#7885; sáu m&#432;&#417;i v&#7915;a

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7883;nh ngày vui m&#7903; ti&#7879;c xu&acirc;n,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vua cho l&#7877; v&#7853;t, n&#7897;i th&#7847;n &#273;em 
      ban.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i truy&#7873;n v&#259;n v&otilde; bá quan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7913; ngày cùng &#273;&#7871;n t&#432;&#7899;ng m&ocirc;n l&#7877; 
      m&#7915;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7911; &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng, d&#7885;n d&#7865;p t&#432;ng b&#7915;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7905; ràng k&#7871;t thái tr&#432;&#417;ng &#273;&#259;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vóc &#273;&#7873; ch&#7919;, g&#7845;m thêu bài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7883;nh ca Thiên b&#7843;o, chúc l&#7901;i 
      Nghiêu Hoa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7853;p dìu ch&#7889;n múa n&#417;i ca,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trò bày bách hí, nh&#7883;p hoà bát 
      &acirc;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chong giá n&#7871;n, qu&#7841;t lò tr&#7847;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Rót bình r&#432;&#7907;u ng&#7885;c, d&acirc;ng m&acirc;m 
      &#273;ào vàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&otilde;ng &#273;&#7847;y c&#7917;a, ng&#7921;a chen &#273;àng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&#7855;p tri&#7873;u quan &#273;&#7871;n, kh&#7855;p 
      hoàng th&acirc;n ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bày ph&ocirc; chén ng&#7885;c, &#273;&#361;a ngà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gia hào m&#7845;y b&#7913;c, tr&acirc;n la m&#7885;i 
      mùi</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#432;&#7899;ng c&ocirc;ng th&#259;m th&#7859;m xa ng&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7871;p t&acirc;n phó m&#7863;c con nu&ocirc;i h&#7885; 
      Hoàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng l&#7877; v&#7853;t t&#7847;m th&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mi&#7871;n vài c&acirc;n v&#7899;i l&#7841;p hoàng vài 
      &#273;&ocirc;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoàng Tung tr&ocirc;ng th&#7845;y m&#7881;m 
      c&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tìm L&#432; c&ocirc;ng &#273;&#7871;n t&#7853;n n&#417;i ti&#7871;n 
      trình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#432;&#7899;ng c&ocirc;ng ng&#7891;i tr&#432;&#7899;c th&#7885; 
      bình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chói vàng bu&ocirc;ng m&#361;, ng&#7855;t xanh 
      hoa bào.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#432; b&#7873; tr&#432;&#7899;ng g&#7845;m màn &#273;ào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mùi h&#432;&#417;ng x&#7841; ngát, ti&#7871;ng thi&#7873;u 
      nh&#7841;c rung.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng xa nhác th&#7845;y Hoàng Tung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xem &#273;&#417;n l&#7877; m&#7899;i thung dung d&#7841;y 
      l&#7901;i:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&Iacute;t nhi&#7873;u có l&#7877; thì th&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&atilde;y thu l&#7845;y &#273;&#7845;y, khuyên m&#7901;i 
      h&#7859;n hoi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tay này là b&#7853;c &#273;&#7841;i tài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7875; ta mua chu&#7897;c làm t&ocirc;i t&#7899; 
      nhà.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7913; l&#7901;i, Tung m&#7899;i b&#432;&#7899;c ra,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chào r&#7857;ng: «V&acirc;ng m&#7879;nh &acirc;n gia 
      m&#7901;i ng&#7891;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#7877; &#273;&#417;n &#273;&acirc;y &#273;&atilde; thu r&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Dám khuyên vài chén ti&#7879;c vui 
      g&#7885;i là!</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «Nh&#7919;ng &#7903; xa,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7859;ng hay qui ch&#7913;c tu&#7893;i &#273;à bao

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tung r&#7857;ng: «Tu&#7893;i c&#361;ng ch&#432;a

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Các quan bi&#7871;t &yacute; Hoàng Tung,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7845;t thình lình b&#7895;ng trong bàn

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#432; mèo tiu ng&#7881;u m&#7845;t tai,</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vào trong t&#7913;c t&#7889;c m&#7897;t h&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bên màn L&#432; K&#7927;, k&#7873; ng&#7891;i n&#7881; non.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7887; to chua cái, chua con:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&ocirc;m x&#432;a ki&#7879;u &#273;&#7871;n nghi m&ocirc;n còn

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432; r&#7857;ng: «con ch&#7899; lo chi,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ra tay, r&#7891;i s&#7869; li&#7879;u k&#7923; b&#7867;

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bè gian &#273;&#432;&#417;ng s&#7855;p m&#432;u l&#7915;a,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7895;ng &#273;&acirc;u n&#7897;i giám &#273;&atilde; ra truy&#7873;n

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Li&#7873;n tay th&#7843;o s&#7899; m&#7897;t bài,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Theo ch&acirc;n n&#7897;i giám vào n&#417;i &#273;&#7873;n

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n &#273;em chính pháp th&#7883; tào,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bu&#7893;i ch&#7847;u v&#7915;a r&#7841;ng ngày mai,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#432;&#7901;ng hoàng r&#7857;ng:

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7883;nh ngày ti&#7871;n th&#7843;o ng&otilde; h&#7847;u

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7867; quy&#7873;n tham tán, ng&#432;&#7901;i

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«D&#7865;p yên khói gi&#7863;c, quét thanh
