      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai ng&#432;&#7901;i nghe nói c&#259;n do,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c th&#7873;m ph&#7911; ph&#7909;c, m&#432;&#7899;t m&#7891; 
      h&ocirc;i l&#432;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng ra tr&#432;&#7899;c t&acirc;u r&#7857;ng:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Dám bày lòng ki&#7871;n xin d&acirc;ng b&#7879; 
      r&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Phen này &#273;&#7897;ng vi&#7879;c binh 
      nhung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&#361;ng vì L&#432; K&#7927;, Hoàng Tung hai 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Khéo là bày vi&#7879;c trêu ng&#432;&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&#7845;m c&#417; ch&#7849;n m&#7877;, c&#432;&#7899;p m&#7891;i 
      ti&#7875;u di,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Thóc kho, c&#7911;a n&#432;&#7899;c thi&#7871;u chi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xin cho ch&#7849;n th&#7843;i theo y l&#7879; 
      th&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#7845;y &#273;&#7847;u hai g&atilde; L&#432;, Hoàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#7855;t là Thát tr&#7903; v&#7873; hàng ch&#7859;ng 
      sai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Can chi g&acirc;y vi&#7879;c c&otilde;i ngoài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;em tài qua giáp ép ng&#432;&#7901;i v&#259;n 
      ch&#432;&#417;ng.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vua &#272;&#432;&#7901;ng n&#7893;i gi&#7853;n v&#7897;i vàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7857;ng: «Vì gi&#7863;c Thát tìm &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      hoàn binh.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kíp truy&#7873;n &#273;ao ph&#7911; chính hình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i hay t&#7845;m d&#7841; trung trinh khác th&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&atilde;y còn ng&#7843;nh l&#7841;i tri&#7873;u &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mi&#7879;ng còn s&#7881; nh&#7909;c L&#432;, Hoàng 
      ch&#432;a th&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;&#417;ng thay trung ngh&#297;a nh&#432; 
      ai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sa c&#417; m&#7897;t phút ra ng&#432;&#7901;i c&#7917;u 
      nguyên</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n &#272;&ocirc;ng S&#417; v&#7899;i L&#7841;c Thiên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7841; l&acirc;y c&#361;ng ph&#7843;i cách quy&#7873;n 
      h&#7891;i d&acirc;n </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai ng&#432;&#7901;i th&#432;&#417;ng k&#7867; trung 
      th&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297; tình bè b&#7841;n &acirc;n c&#7847;n th&#7903; 
      than.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng Mai B&#7841;ch m&#7899;i lo toan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vào chùa T&#432;&#7899;ng qu&#7889;c bàn hoàn 
      v&#7899;i s&#432;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xin &#273;em linh c&#7919;u &#273;&#7875; nh&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7909;c chàng Mai B&#7841;ch v&#7873; nhà &#273;&#432;a 
      tin.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i hay t&#7909;c ng&#7919; th&#7871; truy&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7901;i r&#7857;ng «ho&#7841; chí v&ocirc; &#273;&#417;n» c&#361;ng 
      th&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lòng chua xót, b&#432;&#7899;c phong 
      s&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7917;a &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng B&#7841;ch c&#361;ng su&#7889;i vàng 
      ch&#417;i xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-weight: 700; font-family: Times New Roman">II</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432; c&ocirc;ng gi&#7843; ch&#7881; sai ra,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7871;n Th&#432;&#7901;ng-ch&acirc;u n&atilde; m&#7897;t nhà h&#7885; 
      Mai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ru&#7893;i mau l&#7879;nh ti&#7877;n ho&#7843; bài</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7845;y ngày tho&#7855;t &#273;&atilde; t&#7899;i n&#417;i ch&acirc;u 
      Th&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Còn &#273;&#432;&#417;ng trách c&#7913; ph&#7911; &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bi&#7871;t tình may có m&#7897;t chàng &#272;&#7891; 
      Th&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;êm khuya kíp v&#7897;i d&#7901;i ch&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mách c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; v&#7899;i phu nh&acirc;n li&#7879;u 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7865; con xi&#7871;t n&#7895;i kinh hoàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng xu&ocirc;i n&#7895;i th&#7843;m, v&#7897;i vàng 
      tr&#7889;n &#273;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chia &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng tìm ch&#7889;n tr&#7907; thì,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7905; khi g&#7845;p khúc, &#273;&#7905; khi v&#7853;n 
      cùng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n sang &#273;&#7845;t S&#417;n-&#273;&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Có em l&#7925; &#273;ó c&#361;ng trong &#273;&#7891;ng 
      bào.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7891; Th&acirc;n lòng xi&#7871;t ngh&#297;a sao,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#432;&#7903;ng &acirc;n ngh&#297;a tr&#432;&#7899;c xin theo 
      &#273;i cùng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7917;a sang hành l&yacute; v&#7915;a xong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; v&#7899;i H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng b&#432;&#7899;c ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghi-tr&#432;ng huy&#7879;n &#7845;y ch&#7859;ng xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7841;c th&acirc;n l&#7925; &#273;ó tên là H&#7847;u 
      loan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7841;i ng&#7847;n g&#7863;p b&#432;&#7899;c gian nan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">ng&#432;&#7901;i b&acirc;ng khu&acirc;ng m&#7865;, k&#7867; bàn 
      hoàn con.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i &#273;à m&#7903; l&#7889;i sinh m&ocirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7891;ng v&#7873; bi&#7875;n, c&#7885;p v&#7873; non bao 
      gi&#7901;?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7911; binh khen khéo h&#7919;ng h&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Canh n&#259;m v&#7915;a kéo &#273;&#7871;n nhà h&#7885; 
      Mai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c sau nào th&#7845;y m&#7897;t ai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khói nhà l&#7841;nh ng&#7855;t, ti&#7871;ng 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i v&#7855;ng tanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sai nha nó nh&#7919;ng nghi tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7843;o nhau t&#7847;m n&atilde; chung quanh 
      trong làng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n ph&#7853;n &#7845;y &#273;&atilde; an,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#361;ng may &#273;&#432;&#7907;c ch&#7889;n nhà em 
      n&#432;&#417;ng mình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;&#417;ng thay c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; tu&#7893;i xanh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Long &#273;ong m&#7845;y h&#7897;i, g&#7853;p gh&#7873;nh 
      m&#7845;y phen.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7915; khi th&#7847;y t&#7899; xu&#7889;ng thuy&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thu&#7853;n dòng thu&#7927; &#273;&#7841;o t&#7899;i mi&#7873;n 
      Nghi-tr&#432;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7899;i n&#417;i lên b&#7897; dùng d&#7857;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vào n&#417;i ph&#7841;n &#273;i&#7871;m h&#7887;i ch&#7915;ng 
      tr&#432;&#7899;c sau:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#7841; lùng xin t&#7887; cho nhau,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Huy&#7879;n quan có ph&#7843;i h&#7885; H&#7847;u tên 
      Loan?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nhà hàng th&#7845;y khách h&#7887;i han,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trình r&#7857;ng: «Th&ocirc;i ch&#7899; h&#7887;i bàn 
      làm chi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Lòng ng&#432;&#7901;i &#259;n x&#7893;i &#7903; thì,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«N&#7863;ng bên danh l&#7907;i, nh&#7865; b&#7873; 
      th&acirc;n h&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Cho hay giàu &#273;i&#7871;c, sang &#273;ui,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tìm vào tr&#432;&#7899;c &#273;&atilde; l&#7855;m ng&#432;&#7901;i ra 
      kh&ocirc;ng.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghe th&ocirc;i c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; ng&#7841;i ngùng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7891;i bên khen chú H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng 
      m&#7887;ng tai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;êm khuya ghé l&#7841;i r&#7881; l&#7901;i:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Lòng ng&#432;&#7901;i nham hi&#7875;m, thói 
      &#273;&#7901;i viêm l&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7899;i r&#7891;i nghe chuy&#7879;n nhà hàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&#7885; H&#7847;u &#273;&acirc;y c&#361;ng là ph&#432;&#7901;ng 
      l&#259;ng nh&#259;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Mà ta lánh n&#7841;n b&#259;ng ch&#7915;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Chúng t&ocirc;i d&#7841;i d&#7841; ngh&#297; &#273;&#7915;ng 
      v&#7897;i tin.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7845;p kinh n&#7871;u ch&#7859;ng tòng 
      quy&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«S&#7907; khi mu&ocirc;n m&#7897;t chu tuy&#7873;n làm 
      sao?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&ocirc;i xin thay m&#7863;t tr&#432;&#7899;c vào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nghe binh tình &#7903; th&#7871; nào th&#7917; 
      xem.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&#7857;ng ra lòng cá d&#7841; chim,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&ocirc;i &#273;&#432;&#417;ng n&#7841;n &#7845;y, ng&#432;&#7901;i tìm 
      n&#7867;o xa.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Túi hành l&yacute; g&#7917;i vào nhà,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7899; th&#7847;y &#273;&#7893;i áo b&#432;&#7899;c ra lên 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841; lùng l&#7851;n l&#7897;n hèn sang,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng &#273;i tr&#432;&#7899;c, &#273;&#7875; chàng 
      theo sau.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7891;ng &#273;à li&#7879;u tr&#432;&#7899;c m&#432;u s&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mua tì s&#432;&#417;ng &#273;&atilde; bu&#7897;c &#273;&#7847;u gi&#7843;i l&#432;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c vào c&#7917;a huy&#7879;n Nghi-tr&#432;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7875; c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; ch&#7921;c nghe ch&#7915;ng m&#7897;t 
      n&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7853;y ng&#432;&#7901;i m&ocirc;n l&#7841;i th&#432;a l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#432;a tin bán t&#7917; h&#7885; Mai sang 
      h&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n cho vào t&#7853;n trong l&#7847;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7847;u Loan h&#7887;i h&#7871;t gót &#273;&#7847;u v&acirc;n 
      v&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng th&#432;a chuy&#7879;n xa g&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7895;i nhà oan kh&#7889;c, n&#7895;i th&acirc;n l&#7841;c 
      loài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nói th&ocirc;i gi&#7843; cách s&#7909;t sùi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nào hay H&#7847;u th&#7883; là ng&#432;&#7901;i b&#7841;c 
      &#273;en.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7913;t l&#7901;i tr&#7903; m&#7863;t qu&#7903; li&#7873;n:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7897;i nh&acirc;n ai d&#7877; có quy&#7873;n dám 
      dong?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Con ta y&#7875;u &#273;i&#7879;u khuê phòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Có T&acirc;y t&#7917; &#273;ó, thi&#7871;u &#273;&ocirc;ng sàng 
      nào!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#7902; &#273;&acirc;y m&#432;&#7901;i m&#7855;t tr&ocirc;ng vào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«R&otilde; ràng án &#7845;y, tha cho sao 
      &#273;ành.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">kíp truy&#7873;n ng&#7909;c t&#7889;t giao canh:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«R&#7891;i &#273;&acirc;y ta s&#7869; ti&#7871;n kinh cho 
      r&#7891;i.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7845;y gi&#7901; c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; &#273;&#7913;ng ngoài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng vào &#273;&atilde; th&#7845;y t&#417;i b&#7901;i &#273;i&#7879;u

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quá th&#432;&#417;ng nh&#432;&#7901;ng mu&#7889;n h&#7887;i

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng li&#7871;c m&#7855;t xa &#273;&#432;a &yacute;

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong mình s&#7861;n gói tì s&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7903; ra nu&#7889;t &#7921;c quy&#7871;t &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297; ng&#432;&#7901;i con tr&#7867; th&#432;&#417;ng

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297;a th&#7847;y t&#7899; n&#7863;ng, th&acirc;n mình

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7919;ng r&#7857;ng Mai th&#7883; th&acirc;n vong,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7843; hình, ai bi&#7871;t H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng là

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t &#273;oàn ng&#7909;c t&#7889;t t&#417;i b&#7901;i,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khiêng ra c&#7917;a b&#7855;c, táng ngoài 
      &#273;&#7891;ng kh&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh theo hút xa tr&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vi&#7871;ng th&#259;m, làm d&#7845;u, m&#7899;i phong

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297; &#273;i ngh&#297; l&#7841;i t&#7847;n ng&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tay kh&ocirc;ng &#273;&#7845;t khách, m&#7897;t th&acirc;n 
      quê ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bi&#7871;t cùng ai t&#7887; khúc n&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7903; dang hai l&#7869;, t&#7899;i lui khó 
      lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c l&#7847;n b&#7895;ng th&#7845;y g&#7847;n s&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&#7889;ng thuy&#7873;n nh&#7901; &#273;&#7875; tìm vùng 
      lánh xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7885;n ngày &#273;&#7871;n b&#7871;n, lên b&#7901;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i h&ocirc;m vào &#273;&#7845;y là chùa 
      Th&#7885;-am.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Than r&#7857;ng: «T&#7897;i báo cho cam,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&#7871; này tr&#7901;i h&atilde;y còn làm &#273;&#7871;n 
      &#273;&acirc;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&#7885; L&#432; t&#7847;m n&atilde; còn l&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7845;m thân &#273;ã bi&#7871;t v&#7873; sau th&#7871; 
      nào?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong l&#432;ng s&#7861;n gi&#7843;i l&#7909;a &#273;ào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7903;i ra, sinh &#273;&atilde; bu&#7897;c vào cành 
      c&acirc;y.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;&#417;ng &ocirc;i! sao khéo &#273;ang tay!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&acirc;n này &#273;&atilde; quy&#7871;t v&#7899;i d&acirc;y &#7845;y 
      r&#7891;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i còn c&#7913;u k&#7867; anh tài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong chùa b&#7895;ng có m&#7897;t ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      b&#432;&#7899;c ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lòng lành th&#7845;y s&#7921; oan gia,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7903; vào b&#7841;ch v&#7899;i s&#432; già tr&#432;&#7899;c 
      sau.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#432; ra d&#7841;y c&#7903;i xu&#7889;ng mau,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&#432;&#417;ng thang kíp &#273;&#7893;, gi&#7901; l&acirc;u 
      t&#7881;nh d&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7845;y ng&#432;&#7901;i tr&#7841;ng m&#7841;o v&#259;n nh&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quá th&#432;&#417;ng s&#432; m&#7899;i h&#7887;i g&#7847;n h&#7887;i 
      xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7895;i mình &#273;&acirc;u dám gi&#7903; ra,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sinh bèn ch&#7889;ng ch&#7871;, kêu ca 
      li&#7879;u l&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7857;ng: «Con chút ph&#7853;n t&ocirc;i &#273;òi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Gi&#7919; &#273;&#7891; th&#7847;y &#273;&#7875; m&#7845;t vài b&#7889;n 
      bao.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7903; v&#7873; ngh&#297; ch&#7859;ng tha nào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&#7921;c mình nên ph&#7843;i quy&#7871;t li&#7873;u 
      quyên sinh.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#432; r&#7857;ng: «C&#7917;a B&#7909;t thênh thênh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tòng quy&#7873;n h&atilde;y t&#7841;m g&#7917;i mình &#7903; 
      &#273;&acirc;y.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nhà chùa c&ocirc;ng vi&#7879;c c&#361;ng &#273;&#7847;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«D&#7851;u r&#7857;ng lau án, t&#432;&#7899;i c&acirc;y

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&#7843; tr&ocirc;ng ra dáng con nhà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«S&#7861;n nghiên bút vi&#7871;t m&#7897;t và 
      ch&#7919; xem.</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&acirc;ng l&#7901;i c&#7845;t bút Giang Yêm

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      «Th&#7885;-am t&#7921; 
      thuy&#7873;n».</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#432; r&#7857;ng: «Này bút thiên nhiên</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#7885; tìm V&#361; ki&#7871;m, l&#7885; khen Lan

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t v&#432;&#7901;n t&#432;&#417;i t&#7889;t b&#7857;ng nh&#432;&#7901;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7919;ng mong bóng Ph&#7853;t n&#432;&#417;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hay &#273;&acirc;u b&#7895;ng ch&#7889;c l&#432;&#417;ng nh&acirc;n

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7895;i s&#432; nào bi&#7871;t &#273;&acirc;u nào,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7859;ng là tên g&#7885;i Nh&#7853;t Cao h&#7885;

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thám hoa v&otilde; c&#7917; xu&#7845;t th&acirc;n,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ba n&#417;i to&#7841; tr&#7845;n m&#7845;y l&#7847;n &#273;&#7893;ng 
      binh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7901; &#417; áng l&#7907;i n&#7873;n danh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vui lòng tuy&#7873;n th&#7841;ch, g&#7917;i mình 
      thuy&#7873;n am.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n &#272;&ocirc;ng S&#417; &#7845;y là em,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t ngày thong th&#7843; m&#7899;i tìm 
      sang ch&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Anh nghe k&#7875; l&#7875; l&ocirc;i th&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Có khi nh&#7855;c chuy&#7879;n h&#7885; Mai khóc 
      th&#7847;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh riêng d&#7841; ngh&#297; ng&#7847;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xem trò chuy&#7879;n &#7845;y bi&#7871;t t&acirc;m 
      tích r&#7891;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai &ocirc;ng tìm h&#7913;ng mua vui,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7855;t tay, sánh b&#432;&#7899;c, ch&acirc;n d&#7901;i 
      v&#432;&#7901;n sau.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xem hoa v&#7915;a &yacute;, g&#7853;t &#273;&#7847;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trình anh m&#7899;i k&#7875; m&#7845;y c&acirc;u n&#7857;n 
      nì:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#432;&#7901;i n&#259;m theo vi&#7879;c v&#432;&#417;ng k&#7923;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«S&acirc;n &#273;ào m&#7845;y l&#7899;p, &#273;&ocirc;ng ly b&#7887; 
      rèm.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«G&#7889;c c&#7857;n, c&#7895;i r&#7853;m, khó xem,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&#7887; c&acirc;y ví v&#7899;i am thuy&#7873;n kém

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#432; r&#7857;ng: «Anh c&#361;ng là may,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ngày x&#432;a &#273;&#432;&#7907;c chú ti&#7875;u này 
      b&#7895;ng kh&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&#7885; V&#432;&#417;ng tên g&#7885;i H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Bút nghiên tay gi&#7887;i, nghi 
      dung con nhà.</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Vi&#7879;c quen s&#7917;a c&#7843;nh, vun hoa,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Cho v&#7873; bên &#7845;y n&#432;&#417;ng nh&#7901; c&#361;ng

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh bèn l&#7841;y t&#7841; th&#7847;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Theo v&#7873; Tr&#7847;n ph&#7911; m&#7897;t gi&acirc;y &#273;&#7871;n

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n c&ocirc;ng c&#432;&#7901;i nói l&ocirc;i la,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;òi c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; v&#7899;i cùng là phu

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7875;u th&#432; c&#361;ng g&#7885;i &#273;&#7871;n g&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7857;ng: «H&ocirc;m nay bu&#7893;i d&#7901;i ch&acirc;n 
      tham thuy&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xin anh &#273;&#432;&#7907;c g&atilde; thi&#7871;u niên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&#7843;nh hoa vi&#7879;c bi&#7871;t, bút 
      nghiên ngh&#7873; nòi.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7919;ng xem phong d&#7841;ng con 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t nhà ai c&#361;ng m&#7897;t l&#7901;i khen 
      lao.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh &#273;ành ph&#7853;n bi&#7871;t sao,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi ra vi&#7871;t thi&#7871;p, khi vào s&#7917;a 
      hoa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7917;a ng&#432;&#7901;i ngày tháng l&acirc;n la,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t mình riêng nh&#7919;ng xót xa s&#7921; 
      mình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sinh ly xa cách huyên &#273;ình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t c&acirc;y bóng ng&#7843; &nbsp;m&#7845;y cành 
      tang du.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#417;n-&#273;&ocirc;ng non n&#432;&#7899;c m&#7883;t mù,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ni&#7873;m th&#432;&#417;ng n&#7895;i nh&#7899; bi&#7871;t h&#7891; có 
      ngu&ocirc;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghi-tr&#432;ng m&#7897;t n&#7855;m cát vùi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vì ai nên n&#7895;i thi&#7879;t thòi &#273;&#7871;n 
      ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sau này dù nh&#7851;n có tr&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297;a kia bi&#7871;t có &#273;&#7873;n b&#7891;i &#273;&#432;&#7907;c 
      ch&#259;ng?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thành s&#7847;u cao ng&#7845;t t&#7847;ng t&#7847;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t v&#432;&#7901;n ngày gió &#273;êm tr&#259;ng 
      th&#7851;n th&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hay &#273;&acirc;u con t&#7841;o l&#7885;c l&#7915;a,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chen vòng h&#7855;c v&#7853;n, trao t&#417; 
      xích th&#7857;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Có phen bên gió, d&#432;&#7899;i tr&#259;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bu&#7893;i man mác bóng, c&#417;n v&#259;ng 
      v&#7855;ng ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong v&#432;&#7901;n tha th&#432;&#7899;t d&#432;&#7901;ng ai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xem hoàn &#273;&#7893; lá, gi&#7909;c &#273;òi hái 
      hoa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432;&#7901;i &#273;&acirc;u trong ng&#7885;c tr&#7855;ng 
      ngà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7863;t vành v&#7841;nh nguy&#7879;t, tóc ngà 
      ngà m&acirc;y.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7853;p loà m&#7899; &#273;ính m&#7899; thay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7855;c xiêm hoa d&#7879;t, nét gi&#7847;y 
      ph&#432;&#7907;ng thêu.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">A hoàn m&#7897;t l&#361; n&#7889;i theo,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Qu&#7841;t tha th&#432;&#7899;t ph&#7849;y, lò dìu 
      d&#7863;t mang.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xa xa thoang tho&#7843;ng mùi h&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh tr&ocirc;ng li&#7871;c, r&otilde; ràng 
      ti&#7875;u th&#432;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7889;i tình bu&#7897;c l&#7845;y kh&#432; kh&#432;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7891;n b&acirc;ng khu&acirc;ng qu&#7871;, phách th&#7901; 
      th&#7851;n mai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7911;a &#273;&acirc;u trêu gh&#7865;o chi ai,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#7844;y ng&#432;&#7901;i cung Qu&#7843;ng, hay ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      &#273;ài D&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7845;c riêng riêng nh&#7919;ng m&#417; màng,

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7919; t&#432; &#273;&#7873; d&#432;&#7899;i ch&#7919; t&#432;&#417;ng ngày 
      ngày.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297; mình l&#432;u l&#7841;c &#273;&atilde; dày,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chút ni&#7873;m t&acirc;m s&#7921; dám bày cùng

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i mang cái ti&#7871;ng t&ocirc;i &#273;òi,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&acirc;n duyên tr&#7901;i có chi&#7873;u ng&#432;&#7901;i

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c sau m&#7897;t l&#361; &#273;&#7873;u cùng ra coi.</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n c&ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: 
      «s&#7921; t&#7887; m&#432;&#7901;i«,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7855;c r&#7857;ng Mai th&#7883; còn ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      làm nên!»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7895;i tu gác &#273;&#7875; m&#7897;t bên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n &#273;em t&#7917;u qu&#7843; &#273;&#7863;t lên hoa 
      &#273;ình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trên bày bài v&#7883; Mai huynh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7845;y gi&#7901; tình c&#7843;nh Mai sinh 
      th&#7921;c là...</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tìm n&#417;i &#273;ình t&#7917; xa xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng ngay tr&#432;&#7899;c m&#7897;c v&#7883; cha, 
      th&#7903; dài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&ocirc; tình ai bi&#7871;t s&#7921; ai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7875;u th&#432; li&#7871;c m&#7855;t &#273;&#432;a ch&#417;i 
      bi&#7871;t tình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sinh xem bi&#7871;t &yacute; ch&acirc;n thành,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7895;i nghi d&#432;&#7901;ng &#273;&atilde; b&#7899;t canh 
      cánh lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7899; tên hi&#7879;u ch&#7919; Mai c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vi&#7871;t vào m&#7843;nh gi&#7845;y dán trong 
      n&#7855;p hòm.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ra vào gi&#7845;u gi&#7871;m, nom dòm.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">G&#7885;i là ti&#7879;n chút s&#7899;m h&ocirc;m ph&#7909;ng 
      th&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n c&ocirc;ng &#273;i l&#7841;i xem hoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n &#273;òi c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; tên là Xu&acirc;n

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7857;ng: «Ta x&#432;a nh&#7919;ng &#7903; kinh,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7859;ng hay con &#273;&atilde; h&#7885;c hành

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Rày nh&acirc;n nh&#7883; &#273;&#7897; mai hoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Con &#273;&#7873; t&#7913;c c&#7843;nh m&#7897;t th&#417; ti&#7871;n 
      trình.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Còn &#273;&#432;&#417;ng khuyên b&#7843;o Xu&acirc;n

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng lên &#273;&atilde; th&#7845;y bên bình có 
      th&#417;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n c&ocirc;ng &#273;òi h&#7887;i b&#7845;y gi&#7901;,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i hay &#273;&#7873; &#273;ó là th&#417; H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khen c&acirc;u ng&#7885;c &#273;úc vàng ung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      <font size="4" color="#111111">Vóc trong mi&#7879;ng, g&#7845;m trong lòng v&#7869; nên.</font></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&acirc;n sinh th&#7845;y s&#7861;n th&#417; trên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      <font size="4" color="#111111">C&#361;ng theo v&#7853;n &#7845;y &#273;&#7873; li&#7873;n b&#7889;n c&acirc;u.</font></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n c&ocirc;ng v&#7915;a &yacute; g&#7853;t &#273;&#7847;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7875;u th&#432; l&#7841;i th&#7845;y &#7903; &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&#7871;n 
      g&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7841;y r&#7857;ng: «N&#7919; t&#7917;

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Theo &#273;òi con c&#361;ng ho&#7841; v&#7847;n th&#7917;

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7875;u th&#432; c&#7845;t bút v&acirc;ng l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      <font size="4" color="#111111">Bên t&#432;&#7901;ng c&#361;ng v&#7883;nh m&#7897;t bài n&#7889;i sau.</font></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khen tài nh&#7843; ng&#7885;c phun ch&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ba v&#7847;n già d&#7863;n, b&#7889;n c&acirc;u thanh 
      k&#7923;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Yên xong, &ocirc;ng tr&#7903; gót v&#7873;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng phu nh&acirc;n m&#7899;i n&#7857;n nì m&#7897;t 
      hai:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng là b&#7853;c thiên tài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#7902; &#273;ây ch&#7899; &#273;&#7875; tôi &#273;òi xem

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh thui th&#7911;i bên &#273;ình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t mình tính qu&#7849;n lo quanh 
      vi&#7879;c nhà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">X&#432;a nay nghe ti&#7871;ng &#273;&#7891;n xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7891;n hoa th&#7913; nh&#7845;t &#7845;y là 
      ch&acirc;u 
      D&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&ocirc;m nay nh&acirc;n bu&#7893;i thanh l&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7901;i ch&acirc;n xem th&#7917; ph&#7889; ph&#432;&#7901;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7853;p chùng quán th&#7845;p l&#7847;u cau,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7841;, V&#432;&#417;ng m&#7845;y ch&#7889;n, Nguy&#7877;n,

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7921;a xe, dù l&#7885;ng chen vai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kìa ng&#432;&#7901;i &#273;&#7841;i c&#7893;, n&#7885; ng&#432;&#7901;i phú

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Màn che mái, g&#7841;ch ken &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7847;u nhà vàng nu&#7897;t, rèm hàng 
      l&#432;u ly.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&#7855;p ng&#432;&#7901;i vi&#7877;n khách c&#432; k&#7923;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&acirc;y d&#432;&#417;ng chung c&#7843;ng, Cao-ly 
      riêng hàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bên ngoài m&#7897;t gi&#7843;i tr&#432;&#7901;ng 
      giang,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tàu chen m&#361;i &#273;&#7893;, thuy&#7873;n gi&#432;&#417;ng 
      bu&#7891;m v&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chài lan san sát lá tre,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chi&#7871;c &#273;ua mái &#273;&#7849;y, chi&#7871;c k&#7873; 
      b&#7871;n neo.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7919;ng là m&#7871;n c&#7843;nh noi theo,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi v&#7873; v&#7915;a bóng x&#7871; chi&#7873;u t&#7899;i
