      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phòng riêng &#273;&atilde; v&#7855;ng t&#259;m h&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7855;p hòm b&#7887; ng&#7887;, v&#7883; bài th&#7845;y 
      kh&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tìm sau tìm tr&#432;&#7899;c lung tung.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297; th&#7847;m lòng nh&#7919;ng gi&#7853;n 
      lòng &#273;òi c&#417;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7859;ng hay trong lúc du quan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7875;u th&#432; cùng v&#7899;i thu&yacute; hoàn ra 
      ch&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7903; hòm nhác th&#7845;y m&#7897;c bài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7841;c nhiên c&#7845;t l&#7845;y th&#432;a l&#7901;i 
      &#273;&#7871;n &ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n c&ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: 
      «S&#7921; l&#7841; lùng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng này th&#7921;c Mai công t&#7917; 
      r&#7891;i!</span><font face="Times New Roman">»</font></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;òi hoàn ra m&#7899;i d&#7841;y l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7863;n dò h&atilde;y th&#7917; &#432;&#7899;m ch&#417;i th&#259;m 
      tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoàn r&#7857;ng: «H&#7901;i h&#7905;i Mai sinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Toan gieo cái v&#7841; tày &#273;ình cho 
      ai?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&acirc;y gi&#7901; L&#432; t&#432;&#7899;ng nghe h&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Sai v&#7873; trách c&#7913; m&#7897;t hai l&#7845;y 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7867;o còn quanh qu&#7849;n nh&#7919;ng l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Còn nay V&#432;&#417;ng th&#7883;, còn mai H&#7881; 
      &#273;&#7891;ng?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh nghe t&#7887; s&#7921; lòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">U&#7889;n l&#7901;i thú th&#7921;c ph&ocirc; sòng van 
      l&#417;n:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Mai nay mu&ocirc;n &#273;&#7897;i &#417;n hoàn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#432;u gì c&#7913;u &#273;&#432;&#7907;c kh&#7887;i con 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng cùng.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thu&yacute; hoàn m&#7899;i ng&#7887; th&#7921;c lòng:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&acirc;ng l&#7901;i tr&#432;&#7899;c th&#7917; &#432;&#7899;m lòng 
      &#273;&#7845;y th&ocirc;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7899;i r&#7891;i nh&acirc;n th&#7845;y v&#7883; bài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Rõ tình ông d&#7853;y cho l&#7901;i không 
      sao!»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh ren rén b&#432;&#7899;c vào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7917;a m&#7915;ng n&#7917;a s&#7907; bi&#7871;t bao là 
      tình .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «Sao nh&#7919;ng gi&#7845;u 
      quanh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ngh&#297; là ai, ch&#7859;ng là mình &#273;&#7845;y 
      &#432;?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&#7871; mà bác nh&#7919;ng h&#7919;ng h&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&#7913; cho l&#7895;i &#7845;y t&#7915; x&#432;a &#273;&#7871;n 
      rày.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i th&ocirc;i, sinh m&#7899;i gi&atilde;i bày:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7915; ngày v&#7873; quán, t&#7915; ngày nghe 
      oan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bao nhiêu tình t&#7921; ngu&#7891;n c&#417;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#417;n-&#273;&ocirc;ng chia b&#432;&#7899;c, H&#7847;u Loan 
      tr&#7903; lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#416;n &#272;&#7891; Th&acirc;n, ngh&#297;a H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&acirc;y oan khi c&#7903;i, c&#7917;a kh&ocirc;ng khi 
      nh&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng xa, cái s&#7889;ng th&#7915;a,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7845;c riêng riêng nh&#7919;ng nào ng&#7901; 
      có nay!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «n&#432;&#417;ng náu &#7903; &#273;&acirc;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Thù kia &#7855;t c&#361;ng có ngày tr&#7843; 
      xong.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ni&#7879;m cùng c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; m&#7897;t phòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Anh em li&#7879;u &#273;&#7845;y d&#7889;c lòng 
      nghi&#7879;p nho.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tình th&#7871; ngh&#7883; ngh&#297;a ngao du,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#259;n ch&#432;&#417;ng tri k&#7927;, chuy&#7879;n trò 
      chi&#7871;u t&acirc;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7863;c ai g&#7863;p b&#7841;n tri &acirc;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng bà riêng nh&#7919;ng bàn th&#7847;m r&#7881; 
      tai:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Mai sinh là b&#7853;c thiên tài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&acirc;u v&#259;n c&#7849;m tú, v&#7867; ng&#432;&#7901;i y 
      quan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Lòng ta mu&#7889;n g&#7843; H&#7841;nh Nguyên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Thiên tài, qu&#7889;c s&#7855;c hai bên 
      c&#361;ng v&#7915;a.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7859;ng l&acirc;u th&#7845;y &#273;ó b&acirc;y gi&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&#7843;ng vàng r&otilde; m&#7863;t &#273;u&#7889;c hoa 
      &#273;&#7883;nh ngày.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bà r&#7857;ng: «&#272;&#432;&#7907;c th&#7871; là may,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&#7871; thì tu&#7893;i tác lòng này c&#361;ng 
      an!»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thà la có m&#7897;t thu&yacute; hoàn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ra vào nghe t&#7887; chuy&#7879;n bàn 
      &#273;inh ninh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lòng riêng, riêng c&#361;ng h&#7853;u tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi ra &#273;on &#273;&#7843; v&#7899;i sinh thì 
      th&#7847;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432;&#7901;i khuê các khách th&#432; c&#7847;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngoài &#273;ành &#273;&atilde; ng&#7885;c, trong 
      ng&#7847;m t&#7921;a mai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-weight: 700; font-family: Times New Roman">III</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng t&#417; khen khéo c&#7907;t ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&atilde; xe vào, l&#7841;i th&#7917; r&#7901;i b&#7855;c 
      nam.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Làm cho chí tái chí tam,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7891;i ra &#273;&#7889; l&#7841;i vào ngoàm m&#7899;i 
      th&ocirc;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7911; Tr&#7847;n ch&#432;a h&#7871;t chuy&#7879;n vui,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7895;ng &#273;&acirc;u có ch&#7881; &#273;&#7871;n n&#417;i quan 
      hoài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7843;ng c&ocirc;ng, L&#432; K&#7927; ng&#7891;i ngoài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n r&#7857;ng có ch&#7881; d&#7841;y &#273;òi 
      Tr&#7847;n gia.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7885;c r&#7891;i m&#7899;i bi&#7871;t r&#7857;ng là:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Biên quan có n&#432;&#7899;c Sa-&#273;à &#273;&#7897;ng 
      binh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khói lang, b&#7909;i ng&#7921;a tung 
      hoành,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khu&#7845;y h&ocirc;i c&otilde;i Hán, ch&#7885;c tanh 
      &#7843;i T&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432; c&ocirc;ng d&acirc;ng ch&#432;&#7899;c hoà th&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n &#273;òi con gái h&#7885; Tr&#7847;n k&#7871;t 
      th&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ban cho áo m&#361; Chiêu Qu&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7843;ng c&ocirc;ng v&acirc;ng m&#7879;nh &#273;&#432;a ch&acirc;n 
      c&otilde;i ngoài</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghe ra xi&#7871;t n&#7895;i b&#7891;i h&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#7845;y ai m&#432;u hi&#7875;m, gi&#7909;c ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      sinh ly?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&#7903;i hành &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7883;nh nh&#7853;t k&#7923;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432; c&ocirc;ng gi&#7909;c gi&atilde; ng&#7853;n tu&#7923; quan 
      sai .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7841;nh Nguyên ra y&#7871;t nhà ngoài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7913;a gan t&#7845;m t&#7913;c m&#7845;y l&#7901;i v&acirc;n 
      v&acirc;n:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&#7867; chi m&#7897;t chút H&#7891; tr&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nh&#7919;ng tài thao l&#432;&#7907;c, kinh 
      lu&acirc;n &#273;&acirc;u nào?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«D&#7865;p yên m&#432;&#7907;n s&#7913;c má &#273;ào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Khen ai &#273;&#7863;t &#273;&#7875; cái m&#432;u c&#361;ng 
      già!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ki&#7871;p này kh&ocirc;ng xé &#273;&#432;&#7907;c da,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ki&#7871;p sau qu&#7843; báo, oan gia k&#7867;o 
      gì.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«G&#7899;m tu&#7891;ng m&#7897;c v&#7883; th&#7847;n vì,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&atilde;ng thêu hoài ch&#7881;, &#273;ai xuy 
      u&#7893;ng vàng.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432; c&ocirc;ng nghe nói ngang tàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7855;t sòng s&#7885;c khoé, m&#7855;t s&#432;&#7901;ng

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7843;ng c&ocirc;ng c&#7845;t l&#7867;n vào d&#7847;n nhà

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7865;n thùng kh&ocirc;n nói n&#259;ng &#273;&acirc;u,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sóng thu m&#7897;t lét, h&#7841;t ch&acirc;u &#273;&ocirc;i

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432; c&ocirc;ng gi&#7909;c gi&atilde; lên &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      xu&#7845;t quan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ki&#7879;u phu b&#432;&#7899;c nh&#7863;t, b&#432;&#7899;c 
      khoan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7843;ng c&ocirc;ng m&#7897;t ng&#7921;a &#273;i&#7879;u &#273;oàn 
      v&#7879; binh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;o&#7843;n &#273;ình th&ocirc;i l&#7841;i tràng &#273;ình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bên xe Mai Bích, Xu&acirc;n sinh hai 
      chàng. </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7855;c, Nam m&#7897;t l&#7889;i &#273;&ocirc;i hàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#7843;i quan &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&atilde; chia &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      bi&#7879;t ly.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi sao c&#7917;a các phòng khuê,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7901; sao thui th&#7911;i m&#7897;t xe &#273;&#7845;t 
      này.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7891;n mai t&#7881;nh t&#7881;nh say say,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i trong vài b&#7919;a xem tày m&#7845;y 
      niên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng xa khi khoác rèm lên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vòng thành ch&#7845;t ng&#7845;t, toà &#273;&#7873;n 
      trai l&#417; .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ki&#7879;u phu qu&#7923; b&#7849;m m&#7845;y l&#7901;i:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hàm-&#273;an huy&#7879;n &#7845;y có n&#417;i 
      Trùng-&#273;ài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nàng truy&#7873;n &#273;óng l&#7841;i ngh&#7881; 
      ng&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lên &#273;&#7873;n d&#7841;o c&#7843;nh xem ch&#417;i g&#7885;i 
      là.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Huy&#7879;n thành s&#7855;m s&#7917;a g&#7847;n xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;ài v&#7915;a d&#7885;n s&#7841;ch, nàng v&#7915;a 
      xu&#7889;ng xe.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#432;&#417;ng &#273;&#259;ng v&#7915;a &#273;&#7911; l&#7877; nghi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7879;n, nàng lên &#273;&#7845;y l&#7841;y v&#7873; gia 
      h&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quan qu&acirc;n &#273;óng ch&#7853;t huy&#7879;n 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t nàng H&#7841;nh v&#7899;i hai chàng 
      cùng lên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7915;ng t&#7915;ng &#273;&#7907;t d&#432;&#7899;i, &#273;&#7907;t trên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7855;m xem phong c&#7843;nh b&#7889;n bên 
      ch&#7853;p chùng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cao cao th&ocirc;i l&#7841;i t&#7847;ng t&#7847;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i nh&#432; kh&#7893; th&#7845;p, &#273;&#7845;t b&#7857;ng 
      d&#432;&#7901;ng xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7843;o nhau r&#7891;i m&#7899;i nh&#7853;n ra,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&ocirc;ng nam ph&#432;&#417;ng &#7845;y, &#7845;y là c&#7889; 
      h&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&acirc;n sinh &#273;oán bi&#7871;t &yacute; nàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mu&#7889;n &#273;i&#7873;u t&#7915; bi&#7879;t v&#7899;i chàng 
      Mai sinh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t mình b&#432;&#7899;c xu&#7889;ng làm thinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7863;c ai trên &#7845;y t&#7921; tình v&#7899;i ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng chàng, nàng m&#7899;i r&#7881; tai:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tr&#259;m n&#259;m ghi t&#7841;c m&#7897;t l&#7901;i t&#7915; 
      &#273;&acirc;y.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Bao nhiêu tình t&#7921; x&#432;a nay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tóc t&#417; giãi m&#7897;t ch&#7889;n này là

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh nh&acirc;n lúc v&#7855;ng ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">G&#7841;t ngang n&#432;&#7899;c m&#7855;t, bày l&#7901;i

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nói càng nh&#432; g&#7907;i c&#417;n s&#7847;u,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Trách vì phúc b&#7841;c sánh &#273;&acirc;u má

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Qu&#7843;n chi tr&#7855;ng tóc, n&#7905; nào

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Phiên bang &#273;&#7871;n &#273;&#7845;y phen này,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«S&#7861;n ng&ocirc;i phi h&#7853;u dành ngày

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Riêng ai trách l&#7851;n tr&#7901;i già,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xe t&#417; sao khéo h&#7919;ng h&#7901; c&#7907;t

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nàng r&#7857;ng: «L&#7901;i d&#7841;y d&#432;&#7901;ng sai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7845;m th&acirc;n &#273;&atilde; phó c&#7917;a Mai nh&#7919;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«S&ocirc;ng dù c&#7841;n, núi dù lay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&atilde; li&#7873;u x&#432;&#417;ng tr&#7855;ng dám thay 
      lòng vàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;em th&acirc;n &#273;&#7889;i v&#7899;i c&#432;&#417;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7841; lòng ng&#432;&#7901;i c&#361; treo g&#432;&#417;ng 
      d&#432;&#7899;i &#273;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Mình nào sánh v&#7899;i tanh h&ocirc;i?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7863;t nào còn &#273;&#7913;ng c&otilde;i &#273;&#7901;i &#273;&#432;&#7907;c

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Khuyên chàng ghi ngh&#297;a xu&acirc;n

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      &#273;ang v&#7915;a.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7897;t mai l&#7897;c n&#432;&#7899;c, ph&#7845;n vua,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Danh mình c&#361;ng r&#7841;ng, thù nhà 
      c&#361;ng xong.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Riêng ai &#273;ành tr&#7885;n ch&#7919; tòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Su&#7889;i vàng t&#432;&#7899;i mát, má h&#7891;ng 
      l&acirc;y th&#417;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«R&#7891;i &#273;&acirc;y k&#7867; b&#7855;c, ng&#432;&#7901;i nam,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Cành thoa xin t&#7863;ng &#273;&#7875; làm c&#7911;a 
      tin.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&#7853;t th&#432;&#7901;ng g&#7885;i chút làm 
      duyên!»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7921; tình m&#7899;i l&#7841;i t&#7843; thiên lu&#7853;t 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trùng &#273;ài ghi &#273;&#7875; m&#7897;t ch&#432;&#417;ng</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&atilde; nhau còn nh&#7899; &#273;&ocirc;i &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      bi&#7879;t ly.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh &#273;&ocirc;i gi&#7885;t d&#7847;m d&#7873;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      <font size="4" color="#111111">Thoa thì ch&#7883;u l&#7845;y, th&#417; thì ho&#7841; theo. </font>
      <a href="mk:@MSITStore:E:/Tong%20Phuoc%20Khai/lo^mco^m/NhiDoMai.chm::/chu_thich.htm#th&#417;ho&#7841;" target="chu_thich" style="color: #111111; text-decoration: none">
      <font size="4">&#9786;</font></a></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&acirc;ng khu&acirc;ng l&#7901;i ít tình nhi&#7873;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7883; tì &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&atilde; d&#7853;p dìu kéo lên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng nhau l&#7841;y tr&#432;&#7899;c &#273;ài ti&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7847;n thang b&#432;&#7899;c xu&#7889;ng, gi&#7909;c 
      li&#7873;n gióng xe.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gia h&#432;&#417;ng tình n&#7863;ng b&#7873; b&#7873;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font size="4" color="#111111"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      &#7842;i quan &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&atilde; g&#7847;n k&#7873; t&#7899;i n&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7893;ng binh s&#7917;a ch&#7889;n ngh&#7881; ng&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7843;ng c&ocirc;ng &#273;&atilde; d&#7841;y cho m&#7901;i c&#7843;i 
      trang.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7855;m mình ra v&#7867; Man ph&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xiêm t&#417;i t&#7843; b&#7913;c, áo lang l&#7893; 
      mùi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&ocirc;ng cài trên m&#361; m&#7897;t &#273;&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7893;i hình khuê n&#7919; ra ng&#432;&#7901;i Yên 
      chi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7921;a &#273;&acirc;u kéo &#273;&#7871;n t&#7913;c thì,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sinh g&#7847;n &#273;&#7845;y phù trì lên 
      yên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hình dung ai v&#7869; cho nên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#432;&#417;ng xa &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7893;i m&atilde; yên bao 
      gi&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai chàng c&#7843;m n&#7895;i g&#7847;n xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7873;u cùng &#273;&#7885;c m&#7897;t c&acirc;u th&#417; ti&#7877;n 
      hành.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;Lên yên nàng c&#361;ng g&#7917;i tình, </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7885;c r&#7891;i m&#7899;i gi&#7909;c hai sinh tr&#7903; 
      v&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7841;i ngùng thay, lúc ph&acirc;n k&#7923;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432;&#7901;i v&#7873; c&#7889; qu&#7889;c, k&#7867; &#273;i sa 
      tr&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432;&#7901;i &#273;&#7891;ng th&#7845;t, k&#7867; tha 
      ph&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hàng ch&acirc;u sùi s&#7909;t, b&#432;&#7899;c &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      chia &#273;&ocirc;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nàng còn d&#7863;n v&#7899;i m&#7845;y l&#7901;i</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Thù kia ch&#7899; &#273;&#7875; &#273;&#7897;i tr&#7901;i &#7903; 
      chung.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Sao cho t&acirc;m l&#7921;c hi&#7879;p &#273;&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7893; gan ngh&#7883;ch t&#7863;c, tho&#7843; lòng 
      n&#7919; nhi.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xót ng&#432;&#7901;i tú các h&#432;&#417;ng khuê,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7889;i tình ly bi&#7879;t, &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng &#273;i 
      phong tr&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841; tai m&#7845;y ti&#7871;ng man nh&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7885;ng xa ríu rít, gi&#7885;ng g&#7847;n ét 
      eo.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bên tr&#7901;i phong c&#7843;nh &#273;ìu hiu,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#432;&#417;ng soi c&#7887; áy, gió reo c&acirc;y 
      già.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7855;c qu&acirc;n hi&#7879;u l&#7879;nh càng già,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7871;ng còi l&#432; di&#7879;p, lá c&#7901; ng&ocirc; 
      c&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng ngày m&#7897;t l&#7841; lùng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7875;u th&#432; ngày m&#7897;t &#273;au lòng 
      bi&#7879;t ly.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quen tài t&#7913;c c&#7843;nh v&#7883;nh &#273;&#7873;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      <font size="4" color="#111111">Trên yên m&#7899;i v&#7883;nh c&#7893; thi m&#7897;t bài.</font></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7921;a dong m&#7845;y kho&#7843;ng d&#7863;m 
      kh&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Núi &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&atilde; th&#7845;y g&#7847;n n&#417;i di&#7879;n ti&#7873;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Man binh quì tr&#432;&#7899;c th&#432;a lên:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Núi Hà-lan &#7845;y có &#273;&#7873; T&ocirc; qu&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c vào xem t&#432;&#7907;ng truy&#7873;n 
      th&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7863;t tròn khu&ocirc;n nguy&#7879;t, m&ocirc;i 
      nhu&#7847;n v&#7867; &#273;an.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7845;c lòng kính k&#7867; trung can,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      <font size="4" color="#111111">Li&#7873;n tay v&#7883;nh mi&#7871;u, v&#7883;nh s&#417;n hai bài.</font></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7847;n l&#7847;n gió t&#7889;i s&#432;&#417;ng mai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#432;&#7901;ng &#273;i tho&#7855;t &#273;&atilde; t&#7899;i n&#417;i &#273;&#7841;i 
      hà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7887;i ra m&#7899;i bi&#7871;t r&#7857;ng là,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#7845;y s&ocirc;ng h&#7855;c thu&#7927;, m&#7897;t bà Chiêu 
      Qu&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">nàng bèn truy&#7873;n g&#7885;i th&#7893; nh&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;em hòm áo, phó doành ng&acirc;n t&#7913;c 
      thì.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Yên xong m&#7899;i b&#7843;o th&#7883; tì,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&atilde; thay H&#7891; ph&#7909;c &#273;&#7875; chi áo này.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ném theo m&#7897;t khúc s&ocirc;ng &#273;&#7847;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoa tr&ocirc;i m&#7863;t n&#432;&#7899;c, b&#432;&#7899;m bay 
      gi&#7919;a dòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vì &#273;&acirc;u y ph&#7909;c b&#7845;t &#273;&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7895;ng &#273;em n&#7919; s&#7913;c phó cùng thu&#7927; 
      h&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng theo t&#7845;c d&#7841; thêm càng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      <font size="4" color="#111111">T&#7843; lòng l&#7841;i ng&#7909; lu&#7853;t &#272;&#432;&#7901;ng b&#7889;n c&acirc;u.</font></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#432;&#7901;ng tr&#432;&#7901;ng d&#7863;m ng&#7921;a ru&#7893;i 
      mau,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng lên &#273;&atilde; th&#7845;y mi&#7871;u &#273;&acirc;u g&#7847;n 
      g&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trên yên d&#7841;y h&#7887;i th&#7893; qu&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;a r&#7857;ng: «Mi&#7871;u &#7845;y Chiêu Qu&acirc;n 
      m&#7897;t toà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xu&#7845;t quan t&#7915; thu&#7903; Hán gia,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Quy&#7871;t li&#7873;u &#273;&#7871;n Ho&#7855;c thu&#7927; hà 
      tr&#7847;m ch&acirc;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ti&#7871;t trinh kh&ocirc;n &#273;&#7893;i &#273;&#432;&#7907;c &#273;&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7845;m thiêng ng&#432;&#7907;c n&#432;&#7899;c tr&#7903; 
      &#273;&#7847;u v&#7873; nam.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&otilde;i này m&#7871;n chút danh th&#417;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«D&#7921;ng làm mi&#7871;u v&#361; s&#7899;m h&ocirc;m 
      ph&#7909;ng th&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7897;t ph&#432;&#417;ng b&#7853;c t&#7889;i linh t&#7915;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7875; n&#259;m, ba b&#7889;n tr&#259;m th&#7915;a &#273;&atilde; 
      l&acirc;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Oai linh &#273;&atilde; kh&#7855;p &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7867; xin c&#7913;u b&#7879;nh, ng&#432;&#7901;i c&#7847;u 
      chiêm bao.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7875;u th&#432; nghe nói m&#7915;ng sao,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c &#273;&#7873;n xu&#7889;ng ng&#7921;a b&#432;&#7899;c vào 
      th&#7917; coi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nguy nga mi&#7871;u m&#7841;o h&#7859;n hoi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t toà th&#7847;n t&#432;&#7907;ng trên ng&#7891;i 
      nghi&#7877;m nhiên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Oai linh mình thánh, dáng 
      tiên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7891;ng màn v&#7867; tr&#7855;ng, ánh &#273;èn s&#7855;c 
      t&#432;&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Son vàng m&#7863;t kh&#7843;m tay ngai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lò trong ngát x&#7841;, &#273;&#7881;nh ngoài 
      bay h&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;nh lùng bát ngát h&#417;i d&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nhìn qua r&#7907;n tóc, tr&ocirc;ng t&#432;&#7901;ng 
      s&#7903;n gai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7919; &#273;&#7891;ng v&otilde; s&#297; &#273;&#7911; ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7847;u trong hai &#7843;, ch&#7847;u ngoài 
      tám viên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7891; th&#7901; c&#7855;m giá án ti&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t bên b&#7843;o ki&#7871;m, m&#7897;t bên t&#7923;

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7909;p ng&#7891;i b&#7889;n l&#7841;y xa xa t&#7921;

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&#7845;n th&#7847;m, th&#7903; ng&#7885;n, than 
      ngành:</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7895;i ch&#7891;ng oan u&#7893;ng, n&#7895;i mình 
      tru&acirc;n chiên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7847;m ng&#432;&#7901;i vì tr&#432;&#7899;c hào Phiên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;em th&acirc;n y&#7875;u &#273;i&#7879;u t&#7899;i mi&#7873;n h&ocirc;i 
      tanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#361;ng li&#7873;u bao qu&#7843;n t&#7917; sinh,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7873; r&#7857;ng quy&#7871;t ch&#7859;ng nh&#417; mình

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thiêng ch&#259;ng thu l&#7845;y h&#7891;n oan,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;êm tr&#432;&#7901;ng cho ti&#7879;n &#273;&#7907;i ch&#7901;

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hay &#273;&acirc;u m&#7845;y b&#7897; C&ocirc;ng tào,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7841;y r&#7891;i phó tr&#7843; th&#7847;n h&#7891;n,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i &#273;òi l&#7921;c s&#297; &ocirc;n t&#7891;n ch&#7911; qua:</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kíp truy&#7873;n man t&#7889;t m&#7845;y l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7885;n &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng núi Nh&#7841;n, kíp d&#7901;i 
      gót loan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phò nàng ng&#7891;i ki&#7879;u &#273;&#259;ng san,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai hàng th&#7883; n&#7919;, m&#7897;t &#273;oàn 
      phiên binh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c cao b&#432;&#7899;c th&#7845;p g&#7853;p gh&#7873;nh,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Qu&#259;n queo l&#7889;i chu&#7897;t, chênh

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#432;&#417;ng &#273;á qu&#7841;nh, v&#7883;n cành oeo,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chim xào x&#7841;c lá, v&#432;&#7907;n leo l&#7855;t cành.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7861;n sàng c&#7843;nh v&#7853;t chung quanh,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phong &#273;&#7891;n m&#7903; qu&#7841;t, m&acirc;y xanh 
      bu&ocirc;ng màn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c lên tuy&#7879;t &#273;&#7881;nh làn làn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng nh&#432;&#7901;ng ch&#7853;t &#273;&#7845;t (?), v&#7899;i 
      toan &#273;&#7871;n tr&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7863;t sau l&#7847;n b&#432;&#7899;c lên ch&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nhìn &#273;&acirc;u b&#7895;ng th&#7845;y m&#7897;t n&#417;i có 
      &#273;&#7847;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7855;t mèo trong v&#7855;t, n&#7893;i t&#259;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7855;n qu&#7857;n qu&#7841;i khúc, m&atilde;ng nh&#259;m nhép hàm.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cheo leo s&#432;&#7901;n núi tr&ocirc;ng sang,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khe kia su&#7889;i n&#7885; ng&#7893;n ngang m&#7845;y 
      trùng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7917;a ngày n&#7893;i tr&#7853;n &acirc;m phong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7845;t ph&#417; l&#7853;t lá, l&#7841;nh lùng 
      th&#7845;u x&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7845;t &#273;&acirc;u s&#417;n thu&#7927; l&#7841; d&#432;&#7901;ng?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7849;n ng&#417; mình nh&#7919;ng c&#7843;m th&#432;&#417;ng 
      n&#7895;i mình:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xót thay &#273;&ocirc;i &#273;&#7913;c sinh thành,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Bao gi&#7901; ngu&ocirc;i &#273;&#432;&#7907;c chút tình 
      nh&#7899; th&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7893;i hình t&#7923; thi&#7871;p thay ng&ocirc;i
