      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«R&#7891;i sau nh&#7855;n b&#7843;o m&#7865; cha,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tr&#7901;i cho khí d&#7877;, ho&#7841; là g&#7863;p 
      nhau«</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7841;nh Nguyên v&acirc;ng m&#7879;nh tr&#432;&#7899;c 
      sau,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7909;p ng&#7891;i nàng m&#7899;i g&#7853;t &#273;&#7847;u m&#7845;y 
      phen.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&acirc;n Anh t&#7915; &#273;&#432;&#7907;c b&#7841;n hi&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Yêu bên ch&#7919; s&#7855;c, tr&#7885;ng bên ch&#7919; tài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai ng&#432;&#7901;i nh&#432; th&#7875; m&#7897;t ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngang tay tú các, sánh vai lan 
      phòng</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7879;t thêu, h&#7885;c l&#7851;n n&#7919; c&ocirc;ng,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lúc chia hoa &#432;&#7899;p, khi chung 
      ph&#7845;n gi&#7891;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7841;nh Nguyên khi &#273;&#7913;ng, khi 
      ng&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khóc th&#7847;m trong v&#7855;ng, g&#432;&#7907;ng 
      c&#432;&#7901;i ngoài s&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nàng &#273;à &#273;&#432;&#7907;c ch&#7889;n an th&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xót thay chàng Bích, chàng 
      Xu&acirc;n hai ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#7842;i quan ch&acirc;n ch&#7917;a k&#7883;p d&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&atilde; bài &#273;&atilde; có ch&#7881; &#273;òi &#272;&#7843;ng 
      c&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7859;ng ng&#7901; L&#432; K&#7927; t&#7845;u trong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thiên lao &#273;&atilde; b&#7855;t v&#7907; ch&#7891;ng &#272;&ocirc;ng 
      S&#417;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai chàng còn ti&#7877;n ti&#7875;u th&#432;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#361;ng ban m&#7853;t ch&#7881; truy&#7873;n cho 
      b&#7855;t v&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7843;ng c&ocirc;ng ch&#7889;ng ch&#7871; m&#7885;i b&#7873;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7845;p thêm hành l&yacute; gi&#7909;c &#273;i lánh 
      mình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;Cùng nhau hai g&atilde; th&#432; sinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7849;n ng&#417; &#273;&#7845;t khách g&#7853;p gh&#7873;nh 
      quê xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i h&ocirc;m x&#7871; bóng tà tà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&ocirc;n quang, &#273;&acirc;u b&#7895;ng g&#7863;p ba b&#7889;n 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lòng tham còn bi&#7871;t th&#432;&#417;ng ai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#432;&#7899;p hành l&yacute; h&#7871;t, áo ngoài 
      s&#7841;ch kh&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7841;m ng&#7891;i tr&#432;&#7899;c mi&#7871;u bên s&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thuy&#7873;n quan nghe có thùng 
      thùng tr&#7889;ng canh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghi cho là k&#7867; gian tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghe h&#417;i kéo m&#7897;t &#273;&#7841;o binh lên 
      b&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&acirc;n sinh k&#7883;p lánh &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7855;t li&#7873;n Mai Bích m&#7899;i &#273;&#432;a 
      xu&#7889;ng thuy&#7873;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chàng bèn c&#7845;t m&#7863;t tr&ocirc;ng lên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7845;y trong khoang gi&#7919;a, ng&#7891;i 
      n&#7873;n m&#7897;t &ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7845;y l&#7901;i sang s&#7843;ng ti&#7871;ng &#273;&#7891;ng:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tên kia sao dám c&#7843; lòng m&#432;u 
      gian?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sinh r&#7857;ng: «&#272;&acirc;u dám to gan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;èn tr&#7901;i xin th&#7845;u n&#7895;i oan cho 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&ocirc;i cùng ng&#432;&#7901;i b&#7841;n anh t&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tìm ng&#432;&#7901;i th&acirc;n thích l&#7841;c loài 
      &#273;&#7871;n &#273;&acirc;y.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nào ng&#7901; g&#7863;p s&#7921; ch&#7859;ng may,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Dám xin xét t&#7845;m lòng ngay 
      &#273;&#432;&#7907;c nh&#7901;.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng xem ra dáng con nhà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n &#273;em nghiên bút th&#7917; và 
      c&acirc;u ch&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chàng v&acirc;ng l&#297;nh bút &#273;&#7873; bài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vi&#7871;t xong ch&#7871; ngh&#7879; m&#7897;t bài n&#7897;p 
      &#273;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «Nh&#7909;c nh&#7905;n nan tri,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&#7913; cho thì ch&#7899;, dù chê dám 
      nài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&#432;&#7899;c lên xin h&atilde;y t&#7841;m ng&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&Aacute;o &#273;&#432;a m&#7897;t c&#7863;p, ng&#7855;n dài qu&#7843;n

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«G&#7863;p nhau d&#7877; gi&#7845;u nhau chi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&#7885; tên sao &#273;&#7845;y? &#7903; quê xa g&#7847;n?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7907; lòng, sinh nh&#7919;ng ng&#7841;i 
      ng&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7843; danh r&#7891;i s&#7869; nghe d&#7847;n v&#7873;

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;a r&#7857;ng: «Xin k&#7875; gót &#273;&#7847;u,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tên vinh h&#7885; M&#7909;c, Th&#432;&#7901;ng-ch&acirc;u

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «Quê &#273;&#7845;y ch&#7859;ng xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Có quen Mai th&#7883; cùng là ai 
      kh&ocirc;ng?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sinh r&#7857;ng: «Cùng &#7903; m&#7897;t vùng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&#7841;n bè c&#361;ng bi&#7871;t có c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; 
      chàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7915; khi g&#7863;p bi&#7871;n phi th&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7859;ng hay &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&#7845;y tìm ph&#432;&#417;ng 
      lánh mình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&ocirc;i nay v&acirc;ng &#273;&#7865;p duyên bình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xin t&#432;&#7901;ng quí tính, &#273;&#7841;i danh 
      &#273;&#432;&#7907;c nh&#7901;.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «C&#361;ng ch&#7859;ng &#273;&acirc;u xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#7902; Giang-nam t&#7881;nh, tên là L&#7841;c 
      Thiên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Cách quan v&#7873; vi&#7879;c bình Phiên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Rày v&acirc;ng khai ph&#7909;c ra mi&#7873;n 
      ng&#7885;c kinh.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;êm khuya tr&#7889;ng &#273;&atilde; sang canh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n &#273;em g&#7889;i &#273;&#7879;m, ngh&#7881; mình 
      khoang trong.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7845;y tu&#7847;n thu&#7853;n b&#7871;n xu&ocirc;i dòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai b&#7891;ng tr&#259;ng t&#7889;i, bu&#7891;m dòng 
      gió tr&#432;a.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quan l&atilde;o luy&#7879;n, khách tài hoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cu&#7897;c c&#7901; trên lái, &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng t&#417; 
      d&#432;&#7899;i lèo.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&acirc;y bu&#7893;i s&#7899;m, n&#432;&#7899;c ban chi&#7873;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#417; ng&acirc;m ngoài m&#7841;n, r&#432;&#7907;u kèo 
      trong khoang</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kinh thành mong m&#7887;i t&#7845;c gang,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chi&#7873;u lòng, n&#432;&#7899;c c&#361;ng b&#7857;ng 
      d&#432;&#7901;ng nh&#432; tên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7895;ng &#273;&acirc;u m&#7897;t chi&#7871;c k&#7873; bên,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7887;i ra m&#7899;i bi&#7871;t là thuy&#7873;n Ch&acirc;u

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&atilde;y còn im &#7881;m khí sinh l&#7841; &#273;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thay qu&#7847;n &#273;&#7893;i áo t&#417;i b&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432; bà m&#7899;i h&#7887;i m&#7845;y l&#7901;i tr&#432;&#7899;c

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7859;ng hay quê, h&#7885; n&#417;i &#273;&acirc;u?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«G&#7863;p nhau xin t&#7887; cho nhau bi&#7871;t 
      cùng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Làm sao mà ph&#7843;i gieo s&ocirc;ng?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Hay là t&#7897;i l&#7879;, cát hung th&#7871; 
      nào?»</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Quê t&ocirc;i &#7903; huy&#7879;n Th&#432;&#7901;ng-ch&acirc;u 
      c&#361;ng g&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Cha làm quan, h&#7885; là Tr&#7847;n,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7843; xem phong d&#7841;ng Ng&#7885;c th&#432; ra

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;a r&#7857;ng: «Chút ph&#7853;n tha 
      h&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&atilde; th&#432;&#417;ng yêu &#273;&#7871;n, dám ru&#7891;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xin v&acirc;ng, nh&#432;ng h&atilde;y xin ch&#7901;,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bà m&#7915;ng kíp g&#7885;i Ng&#7885;c th&#432;,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t l&#7901;i &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7883;nh ph&#7843;i ra chào

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nàng e còn &#273;&#7913;ng, chàng coi &#273;&atilde;
