      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Qu&#7843; nhiên nhan s&#7855;c d&#7883; th&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai hình tú b&#7897; khác ph&#432;&#7901;ng ng&#432; 
      gia.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi vui n&#7843;y phút h&#7913;ng th&#7915;a,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khen th&#7847;m trong b&#7909;ng nên th&#417; 
      m&#7897;t bài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7845;y chàng, nàng c&#361;ng m&#7915;ng 
      vui,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Dáng ng&#432;&#7901;i thanh tú, v&#7867; ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      v&#259;n ch&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7855;c r&#7857;ng có ph&#7853;n giàu sang,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7913;ng lòng c&#361;ng ng&#7909; lu&#7853;t &#272;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      b&#7889;n câu.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7847;n dà quen thu&#7897;c b&#7845;y l&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i hay n&#432;&#417;ng t&#7917; h&#7885; Ch&acirc;u &#7845;y 
      là.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7921;c c&#432;&#7901;i thay! thú ng&#432; gia,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7899;m qua T&#7847;m thu&#7927;, t&#7889;i v&#7873; Hàn 
      san.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ca chèo, nh&#7883;p nh&#7863;t, nh&#7883;p 
      khoan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#259;ng lòn sau lái, gió &#273;àn 
      tr&#432;&#7899;c mai .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7889;n mùa m&#7863;t n&#432;&#7899;c sinh nhai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432;&#7905;i c&acirc;u, s&#7907;i ch&#7881;, tay chài 
      m&#7863;t s&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7863;c ai danh l&#7907;i trong vòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xiêm v&#7857;n v&#7863;n l&#432;&#7899;i, &#273;ai long 
      l&#7887;ng th&#7857;ng </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thoi &#273;&#432;a n&#7845;n ná ba tr&#259;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7843;i ph&#7847;n &#273;&ocirc;ng quí, sang ch&#7915;a 
      xu&acirc;n qua.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7871;t lành náo n&#7913;c g&#7847;n xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Neo thuy&#7873;n gi&#7863;t l&#432;&#7899;i, gi&#259;ng bè 
      d&#7921;ng nêu.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432; bà m&#7899;i b&#7843;o Ng&#7885;c Ki&#7873;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#432;&#7907;n &#273;i&#7873;m bói cá thay gieo tú 
      c&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#432;&#7899;i này nh&#432; &#273;&#432;&#7907;c cá &#273;&#7847;u,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Duyên con thì &#273;&#7883;nh xu&acirc;n sau 
      kén ngày.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ví b&#7857;ng ba l&#432;&#7899;i kh&ocirc;ng tay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Thành th&acirc;n thong th&#7843; sau này 
      s&#7869; hay.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bàn r&#7891;i chia vi&#7879;c làm ngay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; bu&ocirc;ng tay l&#432;&#7899;i, ng&#432;&#7901;i gay 
      c&#7897;t chèo.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7847;m sào chàng c&#361;ng &#273;&#7849;y theo,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7885;c th&#432; &#273;&#7905; l&#7845;y có chi&#7873;u l&#7847;n 
      kh&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841; gì &#273;&ocirc;i l&#7913;a v&#7915;a xu&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ai hay r&#417;m bén l&#7917;a g&#7847;n v&#7899;i ai?
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432; bà li&#7871;c th&#7845;y v&#7915;a hai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297; th&#7847;m truy&#7879;n &#7845;y có ng&#432;&#7901;i, 
      có ta!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432;&#7901;i y&#7875;u &#273;i&#7879;u, k&#7867; tài hoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t thuy&#7873;n tr&#259;ng gió vào ra d&#7877; 
      g&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai tay kéo l&#432;&#7899;i d&#7847;n d&#7847;n,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&yacute; ng&#432; &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&#7845;y tr&#7847;n tr&#7847;n m&#7897;t 
      &#273;&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sinh r&#7857;ng: «&#258;n nh&#7919;ng ng&#7891;i r&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Rày xin &#273;em bán ch&#7907; ch&#417;i m&#7897;t 
      l&#7847;n.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#361;ng li&#7873;u nh&#7855;m m&#7855;t &#273;&#432;a ch&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mà cho tr&#7843;i v&#7899;i th&#7871; nh&acirc;n &#273;&#7911; 
      &#273;i&#7873;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chàng v&#7915;a &#273;i kh&#7887;i ch&#7907; chi&#7873;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thuy&#7873;n ai &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&atilde; ghé chèo t&#7899;i 
      n&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trên mui ng&#7891;i tót m&#7897;t ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghênh ngang dáng c&#7853;u, nói 
      c&#432;&#7901;i gi&#7885;ng quan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t thuy&#7873;n v&acirc;ng d&#7841; &#273;&atilde; ran,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7875;u h&#7847;u, &#273;&#7847;y t&#7899;, m&#7897;t &#273;oàn nh&#432; 
      r&#432;&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khoe khoang n&#7917;a t&#7845;c &#273;&#7871;n tr&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Qu&#7847;n h&#7891; s&#7897;t so&#7841;t, áo m&#7891;i trai 
      l&#417;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng sang nhác th&#7845;y Ng&#7885;c th&#432;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khác nào nh&#432; &#7871;ch th&#7845;y hoa &#273;&#7897;ng 
      lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7863;t nêu m&#7897;t m&#7865;o h&#432; kh&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7841;c n&#259;m m&#432;&#417;i l&#7841;ng m&#7897;t phong 
      s&#7861;n sàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Giao cho &#273;&#7847;y t&#7899; &#273;&#432;a sang,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mua làm ti&#7875;u thi&#7871;p, toan &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      ép duyên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thu&#7853;n lòng bao qu&#7843;n sang hèn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tam bành bà &#273;&atilde; n&#7893;i lên m&#7897;t 
      h&#7891;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7885;c th&#432; ng&#7845;m ngu&yacute;t m&#7897;t hai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong khi quá gi&#7853;n l&#7855;m l&#7901;i sam 
      s&#432;a.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khuy&#7875;n, &#432;ng m&#7897;t l&#361; m&#7855;t &#273;&#432;a,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7875; phong b&#7841;c, b&#7855;t Ng&#7885;c th&#432; &#273;em 
      v&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7871;ng nàng kêu d&#7853;y giang khê,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t &#273;oàn thuy&#7873;n h&#7897; kéo bè la 
      om.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vác sào, qu&#417; g&#7853;y &#7923; om,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lao xao n&#7893;i tép n&#7893;i t&ocirc;m m&#7897;t 
      vùng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7901;i th&ocirc; ti&#7871;ng t&#7909;c nh&#432; ong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; toan c&#432;&#7899;p l&#7841;i, ng&#432;&#7901;i hòng 
      &#273;ánh nhau.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bàn dày bàn m&#7887;ng gi&#7901; l&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&acirc;n sinh trong ch&#7907; may &#273;&acirc;u 
      v&#7915;a v&#7873;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Can r&#7857;ng: «Xin h&atilde;y im &#273;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Kh&ocirc;n kh&ocirc;ng qua l&#7869;, phép thì 
      có quan.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7843;o nhau tìm ch&#7889;n kêu oan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7855;t theo ch&#7913;ng tá, m&#7897;t &#273;oàn 
      ng&#432; nh&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c &#273;i bóng &#273;ã x&#7871; thân,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7917;a &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng g&#7863;p l&#361; quan qu&acirc;n dài 
      dài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;èn l&#7891;ng tr&#432;&#7899;c ki&#7879;u hàng hai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hi&#7879;u &#273;èn &#273;&#7873; &#273;&#7889;c ch&#7919; nh&#7891;i nét 
      son.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7919;a &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng quì xu&#7889;ng kêu d&#7891;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghe, truy&#7873;n d&#7915;ng ki&#7879;u, &ocirc;n 
      t&#7891;n h&#7887;i tra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;a r&#7857;ng: «Chút ph&#7853;n ng&#432; gia,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«G&#7863;p chàng c&ocirc;ng t&#7917;, h&#7885; là h&#7885; 
      Giang.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Khinh ng&#432;&#7901;i hèn, c&#7853;y mình 
      sang,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#7845;y &#273;i&#7873;u th&#7847;n th&#7871;, toan &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      hi&#7871;p ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7873; thuy&#7873;n c&#432;&#7899;p l&#7845;y v&#7907; ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Oan này, mu&ocirc;n &#273;&#7907;i &#273;èn tr&#7901;i 
      xét cho.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Cao minh xin th&#7913; d&acirc;n ngu,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&#7897;i vàng kêu mi&#7879;ng, nào lo 
      k&#7883;p t&#7901;.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7879;nh quan m&#7897;t ti&#7871;ng truy&#7873;n ra:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Con quan quen &#7927; th&#7871; cha, c&#361;ng 
      th&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tên này phong d&#7841;ng ra tu&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nhìn xem ch&#432;a h&#7859;n là ph&#432;&#7901;ng 
      ng&#432; gia!»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kíp truy&#7873;n t&#7899;i b&#7871;n &#273;&#7841;i hà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7855;t Giang c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; v&#7873; nha t&#7913;c 
      thì.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nguyên &#273;&#417;n c&#361;ng b&#7855;t &#273;em v&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ki&#7879;u &#273;i m&#7897;t lúc &#273;&atilde; k&#7873; &#273;&#7841;i 
      doanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hiên m&ocirc;n v&#7869; h&#7893; tr&#432;&#7899;c bình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Giáo th&#259;m th&#7859;m dóng, qu&acirc;n sình 
      s&#7883;ch h&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thung dung xu&#7889;ng ki&#7879;u s&#7843;nh 
      ngoài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7871;ng loa th&#7883; s&#7921;, h&#7891;i còi thu 
      qu&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7847;u bên hai d&atilde;y bi&#7873;n th&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7879;m ch&#259;m ch&#7855;m tr&#7843;i, n&#7871;n r&#7847;n 
      r&#7853;t giong (chong).</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7853;m t&#259;m l&#7863;ng ng&#7855;t nh&#432; kh&ocirc;ng,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;i ngoài &#273;i&#7875;m tr&#7889;ng, dinh 
      trong dóng kèn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t vòng qu&acirc;n kh&#7843;o nh&#432; nêm,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hàng gi&#259;ng giá n&#7885;c, ch&#7853;t lèn 
      túi roi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Giao canh tr&#7889;ng m&#7899;i sang hai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7841;m tù &#273;&atilde; th&#7845;y l&ocirc;i th&ocirc;i &#273;i&#7879;u 
      v&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Giang Kh&ocirc;i nh&#432;&#7901;ng c&#361;ng khi&#7871;p 
      uy,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Li&#7871;c tr&ocirc;ng m&#7863;t s&#7855;t l&#7863;ng nghe 
      ti&#7871;ng &#273;&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Qu&#7903; r&#7857;ng: «C&#7853;y th&#7871; th&#7883; hùng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ra ngoài v&#259;n pháp, vào trong 
      d&acirc;m tà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«án này c&#7913; mi&#7879;ng ng&#432; gia,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ph&#7887;ng nh&#432; t&#7897;i &#7845;y, ngh&#297; &#273;à 
      &#273;áng ch&#432;a?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kiên gan Kh&ocirc;i c&#361;ng l&ocirc;i la:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7857;ng mua, r&#7857;ng bán, r&#7857;ng l&#7915;a 
      tr&#259;m khoanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7891;i ra ch&#7913;ng tá ph&acirc;n minh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#432;&#7901;ng c&#432;ng c&#7913;ng l&#432;&#7905;i, nh&#432;&#7901;ng 
      quanh qu&#7849;n l&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quan m&ocirc;n bi&#7871;t s&#7921; ch&#7859;ng sai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7845;y &#273;i&#7873;u nh&#7887; nh&#7863;t truy&#7873;n &#273;òi 
      Ng&#7885;c th&#432;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7887;i ra m&#7899;i bi&#7871;t r&#7857;ng là:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Li&#7877;u dù g&#7863;p gió, sen ch&#432;a 
      nhu&#7889;m bùn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;òi Giang tri huy&#7879;n qu&#7903; d&#7891;n:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Cha làm d&acirc;n m&#7909;c dung con b&#7845;t 
      tài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7855;t tr&ocirc;ng, tay tr&#7887; &#273;&#7911; m&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Phép vua kh&ocirc;n th&#7913;, mi&#7879;ng 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i kh&ocirc;n che.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7897;i con ph&#7841;t b&#7889;n m&#432;&#417;i bê</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Cha &#273;à bi&#7871;t t&#7897;i tha v&#7873; l&#432;&#7907;ng 
      cho.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nguyên &#273;&#417;n m&#7897;t l&#361; h&#7885; Ch&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7901; mai th&#7849;m l&#7841;i m&#7899;i h&#7891; tha 
      xong.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n cho &#7903; l&#7841;i nha phòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sáng mai s&#7869; h&#7887;i xem trong tình 
      &#273;&#7847;u</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ai hay s&#7921; b&#7903;i vì &#273;&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quan &#273;&#7873; &#273;&#7889;c &#7845;y, h&#7885; Kh&acirc;u g&#7885;i 
      là.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hi&#7871;m hoi m&#7899;i &#273;&#432;&#7907;c m&#7897;ng xà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&acirc;n Tiên m&#7897;t gái m&#7863;t hoa 
      khuynh thành.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#361;ng trong h&#7885; m&#7841;c chung quanh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7889;n là c&#7853;u ru&#7897;t Mai sinh &#273;&#7845;y 
      mà!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7915; khi kh&#7903;i n&#7841;n Mai gia,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n sang &#273;ó n&#432;&#417;ng nh&#7901; b&#7845;y 
      l&acirc;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cách &#273;êm v&#7915;a sáng h&ocirc;m sau,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bu&ocirc;ng rèm h&#7887;i l&#361; h&#7885; Ch&acirc;u xem 
      tình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432; bà &#273;&acirc;u dám gi&#7845;u quanh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i &#273;em s&#7921; tích Xu&acirc;n sinh k&#7875; 
      bày.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xót lòng &ocirc;ng m&#7899;i d&#7841;y ngay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&atilde;y &#273;em tên r&#7875; vào &#273;&acirc;y h&#7887;i 
      t&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&acirc;n sinh th&#7845;y s&#7921; r&otilde; ràng</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7845;y gi&#7901; chàng k&#7875; n&#7895;i chàng &#273;&#7847;u 
      &#273;u&ocirc;i:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7915; khi g&#7863;p g&#7905; h&#7885; Mai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi toan xe ch&#7881;, khi &#273;òi xu&#7845;t 
      quan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi lánh n&#7841;n, lúc nghe oan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng Mai sinh, n&#7895;i h&#7907;p tan m&#7845;y 
      l&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chàng v&#7915;a k&#7875; h&#7871;t xa g&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai phu nh&acirc;n b&#7895;ng khóc l&#259;n 
      trong rèm.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khóc r&#7891;i m&#7899;i b&#432;&#7899;c ra th&#7873;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7847;m tay chàng m&#7899;i nhìn xem m&#7897;t 
      gi&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7841;y r&#7857;ng: «Gang t&#7845;c nên xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&#7871; mà già v&#7851;n còn ng&#7901; là ai!»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&acirc;u c&ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: 
      «H&atilde;y khoan 
      thai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Dám xin m&#7901;i c&#7843; vào ng&#7891;i nhà 
      trong.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Thay qu&#7847;n &#273;&#7893;i áo yên xong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«R&#7891;i ra s&#7869; k&#7875; s&#7921; lòng v&#7899;i 
      nhau.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432; bà ch&#432;a t&#7887; gót &#273;&#7847;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&ocirc;i b&acirc;ng khu&acirc;ng ngh&#297;, l&#7841;i 
      ch&#7847;u h&#7847;u tr&ocirc;ng:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«U&#7927;, sao nhà ki&#7879;n, c&#7917;a c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ra tình nhìn nh&#7853;n, ra lòng yên 
      thân?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432; phòng rót chén t&#7849;y tr&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t &ocirc;ng v&#7899;i m&#7897;t chàng Xu&acirc;n bàn 
      hoàn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7875; lòng m&#7845;y chuy&#7879;n h&#7907;p tan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nhà trong c&#361;ng m&#7903; m&#7897;t bàn ti&#7879;c 
      hoa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7891;i trên nh&#432;&#7901;ng tu&#7893;i Ng&#432; bà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai phu nh&acirc;n v&#7899;i ti&#7875;u th&#432; hai 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i. </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nhìn l&acirc;u ng&#7907;p m&#7855;t làng chài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&acirc;m vàng ch&#7891;ng ch&#7845;t, chén m&#7891;i 
      &#273;&#7847;y v&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7843;i v&#7883; t&#7889;t, y&#7871;n sào t&#432;&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khác màu n&#7845;u chu&#7897;i, khác mùi 
      h&#417; qua.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7843; trong b&#7891;i ti&#7871;p ti&#7879;c hoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai nàng tiên n&#7919;, hai bà phu 
      nh&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7915;ng th&#7847;m con &#273;&atilde; yên th&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">G&#7851;m l&#7901;i th&#7847;y s&#7889; m&#432;&#7901;i ph&#7847;n 
      ch&#7859;ng sai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&acirc;u c&ocirc;ng xong ti&#7879;c r&#432;&#7907;u 
      ngoài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai phu nh&acirc;n m&#7899;i r&#7881; tai vài 
      l&#7901;i:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ngh&#297; r&#7857;ng c&#7853;u m&#7907; hi&#7871;m hoi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;i&#7873;m hùng ch&#432;a &#7913;ng, chút trai 
      mu&#7897;n m&#7857;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nay xem c&ocirc;ng t&#7917; h&#7885; Tr&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Kh&ocirc;i ng&ocirc; v&#7867; m&#7863;t, &#273;ai c&acirc;n d&#7845;u 
      nhà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tòng quy&#7873;n cho ngh&#7881; n&#432;&#417;ng 
      nh&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Minh linh r&#7891;i s&#7869; &#273;&#7893;i ra &#273;&ocirc;ng 
      sàng.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bàn r&#7891;i bèn gi&#7909;c gi&atilde; chàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thung dung tr&#432;&#7899;c m&#7863;t gia n&#432;&#417;ng 
      kh&#7845;u &#273;&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&acirc;ng l&#7901;i theo h&#7885; là Kh&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tên là Kh&ocirc;i, &#273;&#7875; t&#7915; sau g&#7885;i 
      th&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Còn Ng&#432; bà v&#7899;i Ng&#7885;c n&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#361;ng cho nhìn nh&#7853;n trong &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      th&acirc;n h&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432; bà xin t&#7899;i b&#7871;n ngoài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tìm ng&#432;&#7901;i ph&#432;&#7901;ng b&#7841;n, gi&atilde; l&#7901;i 
      v&#7899;i nhau.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng truy&#7873;n s&#7855;p ki&#7879;u theo h&#7847;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7853;p rình g&#432;&#417;m tr&#432;&#7899;c, ng&#7921;a sau 
      ra tu&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7891;i trong nghé ng&#7917;a nom 
      ngang,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phút &#273;&acirc;u ki&#7879;u &#273;&atilde; t&#7899;i ph&#432;&#7901;ng 
      b&#7871;n x&#432;a.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7897;i vàng b&#432;&#7899;c xu&#7889;ng thuy&#7873;n 
      nhà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; quen ng&#432;&#7901;i thu&#7897;c còn ng&#7901; 
      r&#7857;ng ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7901; l&acirc;u nhìn m&#7863;t t&#7887; m&#432;&#7901;i,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7885; ng&#432;&#7901;i khoác nách, n&#7885; ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      v&#7895; tay.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7887;i s&#259;n h&#7887;i &#273;ón &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7847;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ki&#7879;n làm sao &#273;&#7845;y? ki&#7879;u này c&#7911;a 
      ai?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bà r&#7857;ng: «Ki&#7879;n cáo gì t&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Này này! anh nó vào ng&#7891;i v&#7899;i 
      quan!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7915; rày ph&#432;&#7901;ng b&#7841;n &#273;&#7915;ng nh&#7901;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7911; tr&#259;m v&#259;n l&yacute;, &#273;&#7911; mu&ocirc;n th&#7871; 
      th&#7847;n! </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Giang Kh&ocirc;i rày h&#7859;n bi&#7871;t th&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&ocirc;m x&#432;a th&#7847;y t&#7899; m&#7897;t s&acirc;n ch&#7883;u 
      &#273;òn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7867;o còn b&#7855;t nh&#7919;ng h&#7847;u non,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7867;o còn h&#7889;ng hách, k&#7867;o còn 
      do&#7841; h&#417;i.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng nhau t&#7915; gi&atilde; m&#7845;y l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i lên ng&#7891;i ki&#7879;u, kíp d&#7901;i v&#7873; 
      dinh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&acirc;u Kh&ocirc;i yên ph&#7853;n &#273;&atilde; dành,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#432;&#7901;ng xa này chuy&#7879;n M&#7909;c Vinh 
      còn dài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7911; Ch&acirc;u n&#432;&#417;ng náu c&#7917;a ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Aacute;n t&#7901; xem &#273;&#7905;, v&#259;n bài t&#7853;p 
      riêng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tài m&#7851;n ti&#7879;p, tính th&ocirc;ng miêng 
      (minh),</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7847;n ch&#259;m vi&#7879;c khách, ph&#7847;n 
      siêng vi&#7879;c mình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hà-nam m&#7897;t t&#7881;nh chính thanh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&acirc;u c&ocirc;ng h&#7871;t s&#7913;c, thêm tình 
      yêu &#273;&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297; mình có gái V&acirc;n n&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quy&#7871;t lòng &#273;ành r&#7855;p &#273;&ocirc;ng sàng 
      &#273;&#7907;i ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xa nhà ch&#432;a dám ng&#7887; l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tin &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&atilde; có ch&#7881; &#273;òi lai 
      kinh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7887; tin, bàn v&#7899;i M&#7909;c Vinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khuyên chàng v&#7873; ph&#7911; &#272;&#7841;i-danh 
      quê nhà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#259;n kho&#259;n mu&ocirc;n d&#7863;m &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tu th&#432; m&#7899;i g&#7917;i cho bà phu 
      nh&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong th&#432; m&#7885;i n&#7895;i xa g&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7863;n dò l&#7845;y l&#7877; s&#432; t&acirc;n &#273;&atilde;i 
      chàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;inh ninh m&#7897;t vi&#7879;c &#273;&ocirc;ng sàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chuy&#7879;n riêng lai ph&#7909; m&#7845;y hàng 
      d&#7863;n sau.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7909;c Vinh s&#7855;m s&#7917;a qu&acirc;n h&#7847;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7887; &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng t&#432; th&#7845;t h&#7885; Ch&acirc;u tr&#7903; 
      v&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tay cung, thanh qu&#7845;t, c&acirc;y t&#7923;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngao du s&#417;n thu&#7927;, &#273;&#7873; hu&#7873; gió 
      tr&#259;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7873; &#273;&#7891;ng theo b&#7889;n n&#259;m th&#7857;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#417; ninh ních túi, r&#432;&#7907;u l&#432;ng 
      l&#7917;ng b&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#432;&#7901;ng xa dong ru&#7893;i vó c&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tho&#7855;t &#273;à t&#7899;i ph&#7911; h&#7885; Ch&acirc;u bao 
      gi&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n bi&#7871;t &yacute; trong t&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tình riêng &#273;&acirc;u dám h&#7919;ng h&#7901; nh&#432; 
      ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Riêng mình chàng, m&#7897;t th&#432; 
      trai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7891; cung c&#7845;p h&#7853;u, v&#7867; bài trí 
      thanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7893; th&#432;, c&#7893; ho&#7841; bu&ocirc;ng tranh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bên gi&#432;&#7901;ng tri&#7879;n bách, tr&#432;&#7899;c 
      bình phong lan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Giá bày túi sách c&#7863;p &#273;àn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7851;n hiên ngo&#7841;n nguy&#7879;t, s&#7861;n 
      v&#432;&#7901;n th&#432;&#7903;ng hoa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phòng riêng ngày tháng l&acirc;n la,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi bu&#7891;n m&#7899;i gi&#7903; chi&#7871;c thoa ra 
      nhìn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Càng &#273;au &#273;&#7899;n n&#7895;i H&#7841;nh Nguyên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7899; c&acirc;u th&#417; bi&#7879;t &#7903; trên 
      Trùng-&#273;ài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7861;n nghiên bút, v&#7869; ra ch&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7915;a bu&ocirc;ng tay vi&#7871;t, ch&#7859;ng r&#7901;i 
      mi&#7879;ng ng&acirc;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Càng nh&#432; khêu m&#7889;i kh&#7893; t&acirc;m,</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nh&#7919;ng mong báo &#273;áp có ngày,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ai ng&#7901; nên n&#7895;i n&#432;&#7899;c này nh&#432; 
      kh&ocirc;ng?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&#7867; chi th&acirc;n nh&#7865; l&ocirc;ng h&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&#259;m lênh &#273;&#7875; n&#7863;ng t&#7845;m lòng 
      ti&#7875;u th&#432;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Dù khi gi&#7843;i k&#7871;t bao gi&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Phong th&#7847;n chính b&#7855;c xin nh&#7901; 
      ghi cho.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bên gi&#432;&#7901;ng m&#7845;y n&#7895;i d&#7863;n dò,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">phu nh&acirc;n ngh&#297; ng&#7907;i thêm h&#7891; 
      nghi ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng nhau bàn m&#7843;nh trong nhà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&acirc;n H&#432;&#417;ng mách l&#7867;o trình bà 
      phu nh&acirc;n:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Chuy&#7879;n này xin ch&#7899; ng&#7841;i ng&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&ocirc;i &#273;à t&#7887; h&#7871;t n&#7895;i g&#7847;n n&#7895;i xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&#361;ng vì có m&#7897;t chi&#7871;c thoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7867; b&acirc;ng khu&acirc;ng nh&#7899;, ng&#432;&#7901;i ng&#7901; 
      ng&#7849;n th&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Hai phòng b&#7879;nh c&#361;ng m&#7897;t &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Mê r&#7891;i l&#7841;i t&#7881;nh, m&#417; màng l&#259;ng 
      nh&#259;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&#7843; xem nh&#7919;ng ti&#7871;ng tr&#7889;i 
      tr&#259;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«K&#7867; r&#7857;ng chính b&#7855;c, ng&#432;&#7901;i r&#7857;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Chuy&#7879;n này &#273;&#7875; v&#7853;y ch&#432;a cam,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xin dò ch&#7855;c l&#7841;i xem làm sao 
      &#273;&acirc;y.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Theo V&acirc;n th&#432; m&#7899;i &#273;i ngay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phen này quy&#7871;t t&#7887; lòng này cho 
      ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7917;ng l&#417; &#273;&#7913;ng tr&#432;&#7899;c th&#432; trai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;em th&#417; ti&#7877;n bi&#7879;t Trùng-&#273;ài 
      &#273;&#7885;c ch&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7909;c Vinh nghe v&#7859;ng bên tai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7857;ng d&#432;&#7901;ng sét &#273;ánh l&#432;ng tr&#7901;i 
      b&#7895;ng &#273;&acirc;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">G&#7885;i vào, h&#7887;i tr&#432;&#7899;c, h&#7887;i sau,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7881;m c&#432;&#7901;i, H&#432;&#417;ng m&#7899;i k&#7875; &#273;&#7847;u, k&#7875; 
      &#273;u&ocirc;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7895;i nàng mách h&#7871;t r&#7841;ch ròi:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Khi th&#7847;n &#273;&#432;a &#273;&#7871;n, khi ng&#432;&#7901;i

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tr&#7897;m thoa, t&#7897;i &#7845;y v&#7873; t&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Khi&#7871;n trong nhìn nh&#7853;n, &#273;&#7875; 
      ngoài xót xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7845;y c&acirc;u t&ocirc;i &#273;&#7885;c b&acirc;y gi&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&#417; này b&#7855;t &#273;&#432;&#7907;c v&#7899;i thoa m&#7897;t

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Làm khu&acirc;y, xin h&atilde;y t&#7881;nh d&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&atilde; &#273;ành tài t&#7917;, giai nh&acirc;n m&#7897;t

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&#7843; &ocirc;ng t&ocirc;i d&#7863;n trong t&#7901;,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;inh ninh dành g&#7843; ti&#7875;u th&#432;

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&acirc;y gi&#7901; g&#7863;p ngh&#297;a c&#361; càng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7859;ng hay duyên m&#7899;i có mang m&#7867;
