      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sinh r&#7857;ng: «Mu&ocirc;n &#273;&#7897;i &#417;n lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«N&#7895;i bà tr&acirc;n tr&#7885;ng, n&#7895;i &ocirc;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ví b&#7857;ng &#273;&#432;&#7907;c h&#7859;n th&#7871; thì,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Duyên sau ngh&#297;a tr&#432;&#7899;c, hai b&#7873; 
      v&#7865;n hai.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7895;ng d&#432;ng nói nói, c&#432;&#7901;i c&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#432;&#7901;i ph&#7847;n b&#7879;nh, ph&#7887;ng còn hai 
      ba ph&#7847;n .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&acirc;n H&#432;&#417;ng t&#7913;c t&#7889;c d&#7901;i ch&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tìm &#273;&#7847;u b&#7841;ch v&#7899;i phu nh&acirc;n b&#7845;y 
      gi&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i b&#7843;o th&#7921;c H&#7841;nh th&#432;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7909;c Vinh ngoài &#7845;y, &#7845;y là Mai 
      sinh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7841;nh Nguyên nghe r&otilde; th&#7921;c tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7845;t quang qu&#7843;ng b&#7879;nh, nh&#7865; 
      thênh th&#7875;nh ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cho hay nh&#7919;ng b&#7853;c s&#7855;c tài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7843;m vì tình l&#7855;m, l&#7841;i vui vì 
      tình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">phu nh&acirc;n ch&#7885;n &#273;&#432;&#7907;c ngày lành,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i truy&#7873;n d&#7885;n d&#7865;p, m&#7901;i sinh 
      v&#7897;i vàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7917;a sang cu&#7897;c r&#432;&#7907;u hai bàn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cánh rèm làm l&#7877; truy hoan m&#7897;t 
      nhà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7841;nh Nguyên qu&#7889;c ph&#7909;c nh&#432; x&#432;a,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thác mành mành m&#7899;i b&#432;&#7899;c ra 
      h&#7887;i chàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&otilde; m&#432;&#7901;i còn t&#432;&#7903;ng m&#417; màng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7915;ng m&#7915;ng, t&#7911;i t&#7911;i, khóc than 
      m&#7897;t h&#7891;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khóc r&#7891;i k&#7875; l&#7875; &#273;&#7847;u &#273;u&ocirc;i:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7895;i trên núi Nh&#7841;n chuy&#7879;n ngoài 
      &#7843;i m&acirc;y.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7871;t th&#7915;a s&#7889;ng thi&#7871;u là may,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bên kia quan g&#7863;p, bên này th&#7847;n 
      &#273;&#432;a.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bèo tr&ocirc;i, sóng v&#7895; &#273;&#7871;n gi&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; sang Ch&acirc;u ph&#7911;, ng&#432;&#7901;i nh&#7901; 
      Phùng c&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bi&#7871;t bao nhiêu chuy&#7879;n &#273;au 
      lòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xu&acirc;n sinh l&#432;u l&#7841;c, S&#417;n-&#273;&ocirc;ng 
      cách v&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t phen nói, m&#7897;t b&#7891;i h&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&acirc;ng khu&acirc;ng c&#7889; qu&#7853;n, ng&#7853;m ngùi 
      thiên lao.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n khuyên gi&#7843;i th&#7845;p cao.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng th&ocirc;i n&#432;&#7899;c m&#7855;t, l&#7841;i vào 
      ti&#7879;c hoa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tin kinh b&#7895;ng th&#7845;y &#273;&#432;a ra:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&acirc;u c&ocirc;ng th&#259;ng ch&#7913;c hi&#7879;u là 
      th&#7883; lang.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7881; ban v&#7873; l&#7877; t&#7915; &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chàng ra m&#432;&#7901;i d&#7863;m, &#273;ình tr&#432;&#7901;ng 
      &#273;ón xa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngó tr&ocirc;ng r&#7907;p bóng quan hà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7867; bài ph&#7909;ng ch&#7881; ng&#7885;n c&#7901; vinh 
      h&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Giáp nhau &ocirc;ng &#273;&atilde; g&#7863;p ch&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Song song &#273;&ocirc;i ng&#7921;a, tr&#7887; &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      c&#7889; gia.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thu qu&acirc;n h&#7891;i tr&#7889;ng vào nhà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Treo yên vách ph&#7845;n, c&#7855;m c&#7901; giá 
      son.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vào trong thong th&#7843; &ocirc;n t&#7891;n,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t nhà th&#7847;y, t&#7899;, v&#7907; con la&#7925; 
      m&#7915;ng.</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong nhà nh&#7897;n nh&#7883;p t&#432;ng b&#7915;ng,
      </span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#7847;m &#7847;m thi&#7873;u nh&#7841;c, l&#7851;y l&#7915;ng 
      ti&#7879;c hoa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">phu nh&acirc;n gi&#7843; cách l&#417; m&#417;,</span></font></p>

      h&#7847;u.</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&#7845;y nay gi&#7843; d&#7889;i t&#7897;i rày &#273;&atilde;

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sinh r&#7857;ng: «Nguy&#7879;t l&atilde;o xe t&#417;,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«V&#7843; con h&#7893; ph&#7853;n b&#7845;t tài,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chàng nghe l&#7841;y tr&#432;&#7899;c th&#7873;m hoa,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">X&#432;a còn gia khách nay ra th&acirc;n 
      tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-weight: 700; font-family: Times New Roman">IV</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#259;m m&acirc;y nghe chi&#7871;u rành rành.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thi h&#432;&#417;ng, thi h&#7897;i, thi &#273;ình 
      m&#7897;t phen.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bàn r&#7891;i d&#432;&#7901;ng l&#7869; ch&#432;a yên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7845;p kinh c&#7913; th&#7921;c h&#7885; tên sao 
      &#273;ành.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7893; d&acirc;ng quán ch&#7881; &#272;&#7841;i-danh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quy&#7875;n &#273;&#7873; h&#7885; M&#7909;c, tên Vinh vào 
      tr&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;ua tài t&#7881;nh thí, khoa h&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7843;ng &#273;&#7847;u &#273;&atilde; r&otilde; tên chàng M&#7909;c 
      Vinh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7911; Ch&acirc;u m&#7915;ng r&#7905; linh &#273;ình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7871;t lòng s&#7855;m s&#7917;a ti&#7871;n kinh cho 
      chàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&acirc;n h&#7873;, dong ru&#7893;i d&#7863;m tràng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7913;a &#273;eo th&#417; túi, &#273;&#7913;a mang r&#432;&#7907;u 
      b&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7845;y tu&#7847;n gióng gi&#7843; vó c&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tràng-an tr&ocirc;ng &#273;&atilde; tho&#7855;t &#273;&acirc;u 
      t&#7899;i g&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7899; th&#7847;y tìm ch&#7889;n ch&#7913;a ch&acirc;n,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7901; ngày nh&#7843;y sóng, &#273;&#7907;i tu&#7847;n

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chen vai tài t&#7917;, danh c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Qui m&ocirc;, tr&#432;&#7901;ng &#7889;c ai x&acirc;y?</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ba b&#7873; chín c&#7917;a thênh thênh,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7897;t huy&#7873;n b&#7843;ng th&#7859;ng, c&acirc;y &#273;ình

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7853;y tài sinh c&#361;ng xem sao,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#361;-m&ocirc;n ba &#273;&#7907;t sóng &#273;ào xem

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hà-nam nh&#7853;p b&#7841; phen này,

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kìa thì Mai, n&#7885; thì Tr&#7847;n,</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khóc than k&#7875; h&#7871;t chuy&#7879;n nhau,</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7887; t&#432;&#7901;ng ch&acirc;n tóc, k&#7869; r&#259;ng,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; m&#7915;ng ch&#7883; s&#7889;ng, ng&#432;&#7901;i m&#7915;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hay &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&ocirc; sát L&#7841;c Thiên,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chi&#7871;u ra ch&#7911; kh&#7843;o c&#7847;m quy&#7873;n

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bi&#7871;t &#273;&acirc;u truy&#7873;n g&#7885;i gia nh&acirc;n,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai sinh k&#7875; h&#7871;t tiêu hao

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «Gác chuy&#7879;n gia

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&acirc;n khi v&#7855;ng v&#7867; &#273;êm tr&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i hay nh&#7919;ng k&#7867; tài hoa,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#431;u ph&acirc;n &#273;áng giá tam kh&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i hay tr&#7901;i có chi&#7873;u ng&#432;&#7901;i

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thiên &acirc;n ban tr&#432;&#7899;c phong &#273;ình,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ba ngày th&#432;&#7907;ng m&atilde; du nhai,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lòng vua &#432;u h&#7853;u, m&#7855;t ng&#432;&#7901;i

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7867; vang g&#7845;m l&#7841;i hoa thêm,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tho&#7843; lòng b&#7867; qu&#7871; cung thi&#7873;m

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&acirc;n khoa m&#7897;t l&#361; tr&#432;&#7899;c sau,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng sang t&#432;&#7899;ng ph&#7911; vào h&#7847;u L&#432;

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7841; t&#7915; ai n&#7845;y ra xong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7901;i riêng B&#7843;ng nh&atilde;n vào trong 
      gác ng&#7891;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kho&#7843;n trà l&#7877; m&#7841;o h&#7859;n hoi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7853;p dìu s&#7899;m &#273;&atilde; cho m&#7901;i Hoàng 
      Tung.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7843; &#273;i&#7873;u vua tri&#7879;u t&#432;&#7899;ng c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&acirc;u, Hoàng ng&#7891;i l&#7841;i vui trong 
      chén qu&#7923;nh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tung r&#7857;ng: «Hai ch&#7919; khoa danh,</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kéo nhau m&#7897;t l&#361; vào toà Phùng 
      c&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;y r&#7891;i th&#432;a h&#7871;t thu&#7927; chung.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng v&#7915;a th&#7843;o s&#7899; &#273;&#7875; hòng vào 
      t&acirc;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khen r&#7857;ng: «Sao khéo b&#7843;o nhau,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Vi&#7879;c là vi&#7879;c ngh&#297;a l&#7869; &#273;&acirc;u b&#7843;o 
      &#273;&#7915;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7881; e tai vách m&#7841;ch r&#7915;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Làm cho kín mít nh&#432; b&#432;ng m&#7899;i 
      là.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&acirc;ng l&#7901;i ai n&#7845;y tr&#7903; ra,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7865;n nhau ch&#7915;ng n&#7917;a canh ba &#273;ón 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xin cho b&#7855;t &#273;&#432;&#7907;c L&#432;, Hoàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7915;ng ph&acirc;n trái ph&#7843;i h&atilde;y toan 
      &#273;ánh &#273;òn.</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Rày &#273;&#7915;ng v&acirc;y cánh cha con,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gian m&#432;u &#273;&#7913;t n&#7885;c, x&#7843;o ng&ocirc;n h&#7871;t 
      ngh&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&atilde; &#273;ành t&#7897;i h&#7919;u s&#7903; qui,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7891;i ra, ra &#273;&#7871;n ch&#7919; gì thì ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; r&#7857;ng: «E l&#361; chúng ta,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ng&#432;&#7901;i g&#7847;n, còn có ng&#432;&#7901;i xa,

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xin v&#7873; m&#7897;t ch&#7889;n trú ch&acirc;n,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cùng nhau mi&#7879;ng nói ch&acirc;n d&#7901;i.</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7863;ng nghe canh &#273;&atilde; sang ba,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ki&#7879;u &#273;&acirc;u m&#7897;t l&#361; tr&ocirc;ng ra ngoài

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phùng c&ocirc;ng ki&#7879;u tr&#432;&#7899;c thét 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ki&#7879;u sau &#272;&#7843;ng, L&#7909;c v&#7899;i chàng

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">b&#7843;o nhau hai d&atilde;y d&#7865;p ra,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7873;n h&ocirc; h&#7853;u &#7911;ng ch&#7853;t lèn,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai bên ch&#7921;c s&#7861;n &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7847;y,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&acirc;u Kh&ocirc;i, b&#7843;ng nh&atilde;n t&acirc;n khoa

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quy&#7873;n nh&#432; chú, ch&#7855;c m&#7841;nh bè,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7889;n bên tr&#7853;n &#273;ánh nh&#432; m&#432;a,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;m bay gi&#7845;y l&#7885;ng, ki&#7871;n tan

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoàng Tung ki&#7879;u &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7871;n sau,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7889;i t&#259;m m&#7855;t n&#7883;nh, th&#7845;t kinh

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i hay nh&#7845;t qu&#7927; nhì ma,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7885;c trò &#273;&#7871;n b&#7853;c th&#7913; ba th&#7853;t

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432;, Hoàng thoát kh&#7887;i vòng oan,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;ng &#273;&#7847;u theo l&#361; bách quan vào

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khóc than r&#7873;n r&#297; t&acirc;u r&#7857;ng:</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Làm sao t&acirc;u th&#7921;c m&#7845;y l&#7901;i &#273;&#432;&#7907;c

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Dám cho &#273;&#432;&#7907;c d&#7921; khoa này

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;êm ngày lo l&#7855;ng mu&ocirc;n ph&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Di tài nh&#7919;ng s&#7907; &#273;&#7855;c nh&acirc;n là

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Gieo cho nh&#7919;ng ti&#7871;ng hay

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Xét soi nh&#7853;t nguy&#7879;t &#273;&ocirc;i v&#7847;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ph&#432;&#7901;ng thi h&#7887;ng v&#7899;i quan

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Con chi tình di&#7879;n toan &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Bá Cao ch&#7871;t t&#7841;i t&#7897;i gì?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;ông S&#417; b&#7887; ng&#7909;c, t&#7897;i thì t&#7841;i 
      ai?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432; r&#7857;ng: «Sao k&#7875; &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng dài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«H&atilde;y cho xong án Kh&acirc;u Kh&ocirc;i &#273;&atilde; 
      mà!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Bá Cao v&#7899;i l&#7841;i &#272;&ocirc;ng S&#417;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7897;i làm tr&#7903; &#273;&#7853;u qu&acirc;n c&#417; b&#7845;y 
      gi&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tr&#7885;ng khinh là b&#7903;i l&#432;&#7907;ng vua,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Nh&#432; &#273;&acirc;y có d&#7921; chi mà can 
      liên.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «Li&#7879;u &#273;&#7845;y mà xem,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«N&#7871;u còn già mi&#7879;ng thì nên m&#7845;t 
      lòng!»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Còn &#273;&#432;&#417;ng tra h&#7887;i ch&#432;a xong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng ra &#273;&atilde; th&#7845;y quan trong, 
      h&#7885; Hoàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;em vào &#273;&#7863;t tr&#432;&#7899;c s&#7843;nh &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Long c&ocirc;n m&#7897;t chi&#7871;c, niêm hoàng 
      m&#7897;t ch&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ba toà ti&#7871;p ch&#7881; v&#7897;i vàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7845;y l&#7901;i &#7887;n &#7867;n h&#7885; Hoàng &#273;&atilde; 
      th&#432;a:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Này &#273;&#7891; ph&#7909;ng ch&#7881; ban ra,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Sao cho t&#7853;n pháp m&#7899;i là c&ocirc;ng 
      minh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Già tay xin ch&#7899; ng&#7841;i tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Phen này hai m&#7841;ng súc sinh &#273;i 
      &#273;&#7901;i.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phùng c&ocirc;ng h&#7887;i th&#7917; m&#7845;y l&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7881; tai Hoàng m&#7899;i khúc n&ocirc;i k&#7875; 
      bày.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;a r&#7857;ng: «T&ocirc;i &#273;&atilde; t&#7887; &#273;&acirc;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&ocirc;ng cung ngh&#297; chút &#273;&#7841;o th&#7847;y, 
      toan bênh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7863;t r&#7891;ng n&#7893;i gi&#7853;n l&ocirc;i &#273;ình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Bu&#7897;c c&ocirc;ng pháp l&#7841;i, gác tình 
      riêng ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Th&ocirc;i thì tr&#7901;i c&#361;ng ch&#7859;ng xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«X&#432;a nay h&atilde;m h&#7841;i ng&#432;&#7901;i ta &#273;&atilde; 
      &#273;&#7847;y.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nói r&#7891;i n&#7855;m mi&#7879;ng, day tay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng L&#432; K&#7927;, l&#7841;i rút gi&#7847;y ném 
      theo.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hung h&#259;ng m&#7855;ng ít nhi&#7871;c nhi&#7873;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Còn the thé gi&#7885;ng, toan &#273;i&#7873;u 
      n&#7885; kia.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phùng c&ocirc;ng, khêu g&#7907;i t&#7881; tê:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&otilde; kia d&#432;&#7901;ng có thù gì v&#7899;i 
      ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#7897;i &#273;&acirc;u có phép &#273;&#7845;y r&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Can chi mà c&#7913; x&#417;i x&#417;i nh&#7885;c 
      mình.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7863;t còn men mét xám xanh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#432;&#7907;c l&#7901;i, Hoàng m&#7899;i k&#7875; tình xa 
      x&#432;a:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«R&#7857;ng t&ocirc;i có chút cháu nhà,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«L&#7925; Giang-t&acirc;y &#273;&#7841;o, tên là Thái

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&#361;ng vì thanh gi&#7843;n l&#7845;y &#273;&acirc;u

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Gi&#7863;c Hoàng Th&#7893; tr&#7845;n d&#7845;y binh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&Aacute;n th&ocirc;ng ph&#7881; &#7845;y, &#273;&atilde; dành cho

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ki&#7871;n bò mi&#7879;ng chén mau thay</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#432;u kia &#273;&atilde; l&#7855;m, v&#7841; này c&#361;ng

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7885; Hoàng quen nói th&acirc;m tr&#7847;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngoài th&#417;n th&#7899;t mi&#7879;ng, trong 
      t&acirc;m ng&#7849;m lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7855;m g&#7847;n s&#7861;n chi&#7871;c roi &#273;&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Dang tay &#273;&atilde; di&#7879;t Hoàng Tung 
      m&#7897;t h&#7891;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#432;&#7899;c ra còn nói m&#7845;y l&#7901;i:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Thiên vàn kh&#7843;o &#273;&#7871;n m&#7897;t hai 
      cho cùng </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&Ocirc;ng r&#7857;ng: «ph&#7843;i vi&#7879;c nói 
      kh&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Lòng ng&#432;&#7901;i là s&#7855;t, phép c&ocirc;ng 
      là lò.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«B&acirc;y gi&#7901; còn dám quanh co.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Truy&#7873;n &#273;òi Hình b&#7897;, h&atilde;y cho 
      gi&#7903; ngh&#7873;.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7841; r&acirc;n sát s&#7841;t xa nghe,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">K&#7867; gi&#259;ng th&#7859;ng n&#7885;c, ng&#432;&#7901;i vì 
      v&#7909;t roi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t s&acirc;n th&#7883;t nát, x&#432;&#417;ng r&#417;i</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7845;t &#273;i m&#7897;t lúc, l&#7841;i h&#7891;i m&#7845;y 
      phen.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432;, Hoàng bi&#7871;t th&#7871; khôn. kiên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xin khoan roi, &#273;&#7875; vi&#7879;c li&#7873;n 
      chiêu ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai, Tr&#7847;n hai án ngày x&#432;a,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&acirc;u Kh&ocirc;i m&#7897;t án b&acirc;y gi&#7901;, ch&#7883;u 
      oan .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ba toà m&#7897;t m&#7921;c truy&#7873;n ban,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sai nghiên bút, l&#7845;y cung &#273;oan 
      m&#7845;y t&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phùng c&ocirc;ng truy&#7873;n phó canh 
      gi&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7891;i &#273;em án t&#7845;u, &#273;&#7907;i ch&#7901; x&#7917; 
      ph&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kíp v&#7873; t&#432; th&#7845;t d&#7901;i ch&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7901;i chàng Mai v&#7899;i chàng Xu&acirc;n 
      d&#7863;n dò.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i &#273;à ch&#7913;ng k&#7867; oan vu,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7915; gian may l&#7841;i ph&#7909;c thù s&#7861;n 
      c&#417; .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7863;t r&#7891;ng gang t&#7845;c kh&ocirc;ng xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&atilde;i tình oan khu&#7845;t, ho&#7841; nh&#7901; 
      thiên &acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&acirc;ng l&#7901;i d&#7863;n d&#7895; &acirc;n c&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai chàng th&#7843;o s&#7861;n s&#7899; v&#259;n hai 
      bài.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bu&#7893;i chi&#7873;u v&#7915;a r&#7841;ng ngày ma,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#417;n tr&#259;m s&#297; t&#7917; ch&#7921;c ngoài hành 
      lang.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phùng c&ocirc;ng t&acirc;u án L&#432;, Hoàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&acirc;n minh tr&#7841;ng cáo, r&otilde; ràng 
      t&#7901; cung</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vua &#272;&#432;&#7901;ng xem h&#7871;t thu&#7927; chung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phán r&#7857;ng: «Gian t&#7863;c th&ocirc;ng 
      &#273;&#7891;ng b&#7845;y nay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Bá Cao oan u&#7893;ng th&#432;&#417;ng thay!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;ông S&#417; b&#7887; ng&#7909;c, nào hay t&#7897;i 
      gì?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7917;u trùng h&#7841; ch&#7881; t&#7913;c thì:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7885; Tr&#7847;n tha t&#7897;i, l&#7841;i v&#7873; ch&#7913;c 
      x&#432;a.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng oan khu&#7845;t xót xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7859;ng hay con cái b&acirc;y gi&#7901; còn 
      ai?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phùng c&ocirc;ng t&acirc;u tr&#432;&#7899;c m&#7845;y l&#7901;i:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Truy&#7879;n nhi ch&acirc;n, gi&#7843; &#273;èn tr&#7901;i 
      xin soi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tr&#7841;ng nguyên &#7845;y th&#7921;c h&#7885; Mai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Mà kh&acirc;u b&#7843;ng nh&atilde;n h&#7859;n hoi h&#7885; 
      Tr&#7847;n.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phán r&#7857;ng: «M&#7897;t c&#7917;a trung 
      th&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Truy&#7873;n cho hai g&atilde; vào trong 
      &#273;an trì.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7877; r&#7891;i, tr&#432;&#7899;c &#273;i&#7879;n &#273;&#7873;u quì,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7841;ch oan s&#7861;n s&#7899; t&#7913;c thì d&acirc;ng 
      lên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vua &#272;&#432;&#7901;ng xem h&#7871;t s&#7921; duyên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phán r&#7857;ng: «Tung, K&#7927; h&#7841;i hi&#7873;n 
      b&#7845;y l&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7901;i &#273;em chính pháp bêu &#273;&#7847;u.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên, b&#7843;ng nh&atilde;n &#273;&#7913;ng 
      ch&#7847;u &#273;&ocirc;i bên</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ban cho ph&#7909;c h&#7885;, ph&#7909;c tên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cao minh mu&ocirc;n &#273;&#7897;i &#417;n trên chín 
      l&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Suy ra m&#7899;i bi&#7871;t tr&#7901;i g&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">chính tà m&#7897;t b&#7909;ng, qu&#7881; th&#7847;n 
      hai vai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gian th&#7847;n ch&#432;a ch&#7855;c n&#7849;y ch&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Núi b&#259;ng khi th&#7845;y m&#7863;t tr&#7901;i h&#7859;n 
      tan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trung trinh d&#7851;u có m&#7855;c oan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7851;n tr&#417; c&acirc;y c&#7913;ng, kh&ocirc;ng ch&#7891;n

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7873; sau ngay l&#7841;i ra ngay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&#7887;i tu&#7847;n ho&#7841;n n&#7841;n, &#273;&#7871;n ngày 
      hi&#7875;n vinh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432;, Hoàng t&#7897;i &#273;&atilde; &#273;áng tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;em ra chính pháp, hành hình 
      m&#7897;t d&acirc;y.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tan tành th&#7883;t nát x&#432;&#417;ng bay,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">N&#7855;ng bêu m&#7863;t n&#7883;nh, &#273;&#7845;t r&acirc;y máu 
      tà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chán l&#7901;i k&#7867; l&#7841;i ng&#432;&#7901;i qua:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&acirc;y gi&#7901; th&#7871; &#7845;y, thu&#7903; x&#432;a th&#7871; 
      nào?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7847;n c&ocirc;ng ra kh&#7887;i thiên lao,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#7885; m&ocirc;n ch&#7921;c s&#7861;n &#273;&atilde; vào t&#7841; &acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phùng c&ocirc;ng m&#7915;ng r&#7905; c&#7889; nh&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7901;i v&#7873; t&#432; th&#7845;t &acirc;n c&#7847;n th&#7903; 
      than.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7875; con sum h&#7885;p m&#7897;t &#273;oàn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432;&#7901;i than chuy&#7879;n tr&#432;&#7899;c, k&#7867; 
      bàn n&#7895;i x&#432;a.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nh&#7899; nhung k&#7869; tóc ch&acirc;n t&#417;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gian nan là m&#7845;y, n&#432;&#417;ng nh&#7901; 
      nh&#7919;ng &#273;&acirc;u?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bao nhiêu t&acirc;n kh&#7893; b&#7845;y l&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gi&#7853;t mình &#273;òi lúc, l&#7855;c &#273;&#7847;u &#273;òi 
      phen.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khi nên m&#7885;i vi&#7879;c m&#7885;i nên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chi&#7871;u tr&#7901;i &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&atilde; t&#7889;c truy&#7873;n 
      t&#7899;i n&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7883;nh ngày t&#7913; t&#7871; h&#7885; Mai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phong làm Thái b&#7843;o, d&#7921;ng bài 
      li&#7879;t trung</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên v&#7899;i l&#7841;i Tr&#7847;n, 
      Phùng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ban quy&#7873;n h&#7885;c s&#297; d&#7921; vòng y&#7871;u 
      thanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Còn nh&#432; b&#7843;ng nh&atilde;n Xu&acirc;n sinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Làm &#273;&ocirc; ng&#7921; s&#7917;, quy&#7873;n hành h&#7841;ch 
      t&acirc;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7885; Tr&#7847;n, h&#7885; &#272;&#7843;ng, h&#7885; Kh&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7853;t gia tam c&#7845;p &#273;&#7907;i sau kén 
      dùng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7907; Mai c&ocirc;ng, v&#7907; Tr&#7847;n c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i cùng Tr&#7847;n H&#7841;nh l&#7841;i cùng 
      V&acirc;n Anh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n nh&#7845;t ph&#7849;m là vinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khen cho ch&#7919; ngh&#297;a, ch&#7919; trinh 
      m&#7897;t nhà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&acirc;n Tiên v&#7899;i l&#7841;i Ng&#7885;c th&#432;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7849;m &#273;&#7873; &#273;&#7879; nh&#7883;, c&#361;ng là phu 
      nh&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai c&ocirc;ng khai táng &#273;&#7871;n tu&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoàng th&acirc;n t&#7871; &#273;i&#7879;n, tri&#7873;u th&#7847;n 
      tr&#7907; nghi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên nh&acirc;n ti&#7879;n &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng 
      &#273;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Duyên &#273;&#7891; h&#7897; t&#7889;ng v&#7873; quê ch&acirc;u 
      th&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i ban tu&#7847;n thú b&#7889;n ph&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#7844;n vàng m&#7897;t qu&#7843;, ki&#7871;m vàng m&#7897;t 
      thanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t mình quy&#7873;n &#273;&#432;&#7907;c ti&#7879;n hành,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7859;ng tay t&#7849;y m&#7921;c, c&ocirc;ng bình 
      c&#7847;m c&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i cho v&#7873; vi&#7879;c &#273;&#7841;i tu&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hoàn h&ocirc;n ng&#7921; t&#7913; nghênh th&acirc;n 
      m&#7897;t ngày.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7855;ng nghe r&otilde; chi&#7871;u n&#259;m m&acirc;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai chàng vào tr&#432;&#7899;c, l&#7841;y bày 
      t&#7841; &acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xem ngày t&#7913; t&#7871; &#273;&atilde; g&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cho tin báo tr&#432;&#7899;c t&#259;ng nh&acirc;n 
      d&#7885;n chùa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên m&#7863;c áo th&ocirc;i ma,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c vào l&#7877; ph&#7853;t, sau ra t&#7843;o 
      ph&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;i&#7875;n nghi kh&#7855;p m&#7863;t tri&#7873;u th&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t t&#7901; d&#7909; ch&#7881;, ba tu&#7847;n r&#432;&#7907;u 
      hoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7897;t k&#7871;t thái, tr&#432;&#7899;ng r&#7911; là,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;èn g&#7847;n chong sáng, &#273;&#7881;nh xa 
      bay tr&#7847;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7889;ng trang nghiêm, nh&#7841;c bát 
      &acirc;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7845;y m&acirc;m sinh l&#7871; m&#7845;y m&acirc;m t&#432; 
      thình.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7889;ng trung li&#7879;t, thác anh 
      linh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Su&#7889;i vàng th&#417;m phách, m&acirc;y xanh 
      tho&#7843; h&#7891;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&atilde; &#273;ành trung hi&#7871;u nh&#7845;t m&ocirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#416;n vua nhu&#7847;n g&#7897;i, ti&#7871;ng con r&#7905; 
      ràng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7871; r&#7891;i linh c&#7919;u lên &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#297;nh g&#432;&#417;m vàng v&#7899;i &#7845;n vàng ra 
      &#273;i .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7841; &acirc;n vào tr&#432;&#7899;c t&acirc;u quì:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Vi&#7879;c công &#273;âu dám b&#7853;n chi t&#432; 
      tình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«T&#432;&#7903;ng khi lánh n&#7841;n b&#259;ng mi&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Theo ch&acirc;n c&#7853;y có m&#7897;t tên H&#7881; 
      &#273;&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ngh&#297; trong th&#7847;y t&#7899; xét c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7845;y c&#417;n nguy hi&#7875;m, m&#7845;y vòng 
      gian nan.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Tìm vào nh&#7841;c ph&#7909; H&#7847;u Loan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Hay &#273;&acirc;u c&#361;ng v&#7899;i quy&#7873;n gian 
      m&#7897;t lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«C&#361;ng toan b&#7855;t n&#7897;p l&#7845;y c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Gi&#7843; hình khi &#7845;y H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng &#273;&#7905;
