      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Rày nh&#7901; r&#7897;ng b&#432;&#7899;c &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng m&acirc;y,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ng&#7853;m ngùi &#273;&#7841;o t&#7899;, ngh&#297;a th&#7847;y 
      ch&#432;a yên .</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Móc m&#432;a xin t&#432;&#7899;i c&#7917;u tuy&#7873;n,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«G&#7885;i là tinh bi&#7875;u &#273;&#7875; &#273;&#7873;n chút

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vua &#272;&#432;&#7901;ng nghe h&#7871;t thu&#7927; chung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khen r&#7857;ng ti&#7871;t ngh&#297;a, hi&#7871;u 
      trung m&#7897;t nhà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7911;a kho ban d&#7921;ng mi&#7871;u th&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7913;c ban th&#7845;t ph&#7849;m g&#7885;i là t&#7863;ng 
      phong.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7841; t&#7915; n&#259;m l&#7841;y &#273;&#7873;n r&#7891;ng</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7903; ra r&#432;&#7899;c c&#7919;u Mai c&ocirc;ng xu&#7889;ng 
      thuy&#7873;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7877;n &#273;&#432;a xe ng&#7921;a nh&#432; nêm,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7889;ng ran m&#7863;t &#273;&#7845;t, c&#7901; li&#7873;n 
      bóng s&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7891; hi&#7871;u s&#7921;, v&#7867; qu&acirc;n dung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&ocirc;ng l&#7891;ng gi&#7899;i tr&#432;&#7907;ng, gió 
      tung bi&#7875;n k&#7923;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7871;n &#273;&acirc;u quy&#7873;n &#273;&#432;&#7907;c ti&#7879;n nghi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t tay th&#432;&#7903;ng ph&#7841;t, hai b&#7873; 
      c&ocirc;ng minh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7847;n l&#7847;n gió mát tr&#259;ng thanh</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thuy&#7873;n &#273;à t&#7899;i ph&#7911; &#272;&#7841;i-danh bao 
      gi&#7901;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t vài th&#7847;y t&#7899; lên b&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&acirc;n tình l&#7841;i t&#7879;, gi&#7843; mà, h&#7887;i 
      ch&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khác hình ai bi&#7871;t là ai,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;i l&#7847;n b&#7895;ng th&#7845;y m&#7897;t n&#417;i có 
      chùa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7917;a ngoài th&#7845;y có niêm t&#7901;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7911; nha nghiêm c&#7845;m k&#7867; ra ng&#432;&#7901;i 
      vào.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngh&#297; riêng ch&#432;a bi&#7871;t làm sao,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">X&#432;a nay c&#7917;a b&#7909;t d&#7877; nào h&#7865;p ai?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">May sao g&#7863;p lúc v&#7855;ng ng&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7901;i ch&acirc;n s&#7869; l&#7867;n vào n&#417;i h&#7853;u 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7845;y trong th&#7901; ph&#7909;ng m&#7897;t 
      gi&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&ocirc;ng lên bài v&#7883; r&otilde; ràng L&#432; 
      c&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thái s&#432; ch&#7913;c t&#432;&#7899;c &#273;&#7873; trong,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7919; «H&#7847;u Loan Ph&#7909;ng«m&#7897;t giòng 
      &#273;&#7873; bên</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7847;m tay c&#7845;t l&#7845;y v&#7873; li&#7873;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#432;&#7901;i r&#7857;ng c&#7845;m cách nh&acirc;n duyên 
      th&#7871; này!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">V&#7873; thuy&#7873;n, qu&acirc;n &#273;&atilde; s&#7855;p bày,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vào toà sát vi&#7879;n sai ngay &#273;i 
      &#273;òi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghe quan tu&#7847;n ph&#7911; &#273;&#7871;n n&#417;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7847;u Loan mình &#273;&atilde; b&#7891; h&ocirc;i &#432;&#7899;t 
      nhoè.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7845;y l&#7901;i truy&#7873;n h&#7887;i xa nghe:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Viên kia có m&#7845;t c&#7911;a gì bi&#7871;t 
      ch&#432;a?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7891;n r&#7857;ng có &#7843; ti&#7875;u th&#432;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;ông sàng ngôi &#7845;y bây gi&#7901; là 
      ai?»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Loan nghe ch&#432;a h&#7871;t &#273;&#7847;u &#273;u&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Run run, s&#7907; s&#7907;, m&#7903; l&#7901;i kh&ocirc;ng 
      ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#432;a r&#7857;ng: «Có chút gái th&#417;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«M&#7845;y n&#259;m tr&#432;&#7899;c &#273;&atilde; ch&#417;i xa su&#7889;i 
      vàng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7913;c ti ph&#7853;n ti&#7875;u t&#7847;m th&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Ch&#7859;ng hay th&#432;&#7907;ng hi&#7871;n sao 
      t&#432;&#7901;ng duyên do.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Lên m&#7915;ng ch&#7853;m ch&#7841;p già n&ocirc; 
      (nua),</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Dám xin th&#432;&#7907;ng hi&#7871;n th&#7913; cho 
      &#273;&#432;&#7907;c nh&#7901;.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Qu&#7903; r&#7857;ng: L&#7897;c n&#432;&#7899;c c&#417;m vua,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Quan qu&acirc;n ti&#7877;n ki&#7879;u kéo hàng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7841; liêu m&#7845;y l&#361;, b&#7897; &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng m&#7845;y 
      ban.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên &#273;i tr&#432;&#7899;c phù 
      quan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n n&#7889;i ki&#7879;u bu&ocirc;ng màn 
      ph&#432;&#417;ng du.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7853;p l&#259;ng m&#7845;y g&#7889;c tùng thu.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t vùng s&#7843;ng kh&#7843;i &#273;&#7855;p m&#7891; ng&#432;u 
      miên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7845;y l&acirc;u táng l&#7877; v&#7915;a yên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phu nh&acirc;n &#273;&atilde; gi&#7909;c xu&#7889;ng thuy&#7873;n 
      &#273;i kinh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên c&#7913; vi&#7879;c tu&#7847;n 
      hành,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nghi-tr&#432;ng v&#7915;a m&#7899;i nh&#7853;t trình 
      t&#7899;i ngay.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841; quen c&#361;ng m&#7897;t mi&#7873;n này,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sao x&#432;a tr&#7889;n tránh mà rày hi&#7875;n 
      d&#432;&#417;ng?</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tho&#7843;ng qua s&#7921;c nh&#7899; m&#7885;i &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7907;t lòng l&#7841;i &#273;&#7897;ng t&#7845;m th&#432;&#417;ng 
      H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tra ra tri huy&#7879;n S&#7917; c&ocirc;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&ocirc;n sinh L&#432; K&#7927; c&#361;ng vòng quy&#7873;n 
      gian.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sá chi m&#7885;n m&#7841;y s&#7867; &#273;àn,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nhiêu cho tính m&#7879;nh l&#7841;i hoàn 
      th&#7913; nh&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&ocirc;ng lênh nh&#7899; &#273;&#7871;n &#272;&#7891; Th&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cho làm huy&#7879;n l&#7879;nh &#273;&#7873;n &acirc;n t&#7845;m 
      lòng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7855;c m&ocirc;n ghi m&#7843; H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7921;ng bia &#273;á, l&#7845;y s&#7855;c phong t&#7841;c 
      li&#7873;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i truy&#7873;n s&#7917;a r&#7841;p m&#7897;t bên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c m&#7891; t&#7871; &#273;i&#7879;n m&#7897;t phen th&acirc;n 
      hành.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Khóc than k&#7875; h&#7871;t m&#7885;i tình:</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Có c&ocirc;ng danh &#7845;y vì mình nh&#7899; 
      ai.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«Vì ta trót &#273;ã thi&#7879;t r&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">«&#272;&#7873;n c&ocirc;ng ta c&#361;ng nh&#7899; l&#7901;i kh&#7845;n 
      x&#432;a.»</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#259;n kho&#259;n &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng &#273;&#7845;t cách xa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Giao &#272;&#7891; Th&acirc;n t&#7921; b&#7845;y gi&#7901; gi&#7919; 
      coi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mai sau k&#7871; t&#7921; còn dài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7885; &#272;&#7891; nh&#432;&#7901;ng l&#7841;i m&#7897;t ng&#432;&#7901;i h&#7885; 
      V&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">G&#7885;i là ph&#7909;ng s&#7921; khói h&#432;&#417;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#7845;y là tr&#7885;n h&#7871;t m&#7885;i &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng thu&#7927; 
      chung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7891; Th&acirc;n v&acirc;ng d&#7841;y ghi lòng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;em con &#273;&#7875; n&#7841;p H&#7881; &#273;&#7891;ng h&#7885; 
      V&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i tìm &#273;&#7871;n ch&#7889;n ph&#7853;t &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n &#273;em áo tía, xe vàng 
      &#273;ón s&#432;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tái sinh may có &#417;n x&#432;a,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&#7845;u &#273;&#7847;u bách bái t&#7841; th&#432;a m&#7845;y 
      l&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chàng t&#7915; &acirc;n oán r&#7841;ch ròi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#7845;t kh&ocirc;ng gánh n&#7863;ng, tát v&#417;i 
      b&#7875; s&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tu&#7847;n hành m&#7885;i ch&#7889;n &#273;&acirc;u &#273;&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong lòng phép n&#432;&#7899;c, trên &#273;&#7847;u 
      &#417;n vua.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&ocirc;ng kh&ocirc;ng sót, t&#7897;i kh&ocirc;ng tha,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&acirc;n hào ch&#7859;ng l&#7851;n, tóc t&#417; nào 
      l&#7847;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7845;y phen m&#7921;c n&#7849;y, c&acirc;n c&#7847;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trong veo l&#7841;i t&#7879;, n&#7893;i b&#7891;ng d&acirc;n 
      ca.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7847;n l&#7847;n &#273;&ocirc;ng l&#7841;i xu&acirc;n qua,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t vòng tu&#7847;n thú tính &#273;à kh&#7855;p 
      quanh.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7883;nh ngày ph&#7909;ng m&#7879;nh lai kinh,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7899; v&#259;n m&#7897;t t&#7853;p hi&#7879;n thành t&acirc;u 
      lên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chín l&#7847;n &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7897;ng ti&#7871;ng khen,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7845;c lòng c&ocirc;ng chính, m&#7845;y phen 
      nh&#7885;c nh&#7857;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Trên vì n&#432;&#7899;c, d&#432;&#7899;i vì d&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ban cho c&#7921;c ph&#7849;m t&#7875; th&#7847;n &#273;&#7873;n 
      c&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7873;n Kh&acirc;m thiên giám h&#7897;i 
      &#273;&#7891;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ch&#7885;n ngày &#273;&#7841;i cát &#273;&#7897;ng phòng 
      nghênh th&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên thay áo t&#7875; th&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#432;&#7899;i th&#7873;m n&#259;m l&#7841;y t&#7841; &acirc;n kh&#7845;u 
      &#273;&#7847;u.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Nhàn ch&#417;i lui t&#7899;i thi&#7871;u &#273;&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7911; Tr&#7847;n &#273;i l&#7841;i, ph&#7911; Kh&acirc;u ra 
      vào.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7901;i xu&acirc;n minh m&#7883; th&#7871; nào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#432;&#7905;i oanh ríu rít, b&ocirc;ng &#273;ào so 
      le,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#432;&#7907;ng l&acirc;u s&#7855;p s&#7861;n l&#7877; nghi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&acirc;m thiên &#273;&atilde; &#273;&#7883;nh nh&#7853;t k&#7923; t&#432; 
      ra.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tháng ba v&#7915;a b&#7919;a m&#7891;ng ba,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Là ngày hoàng &#273;&#7841;o, là gi&#7901; 
      thiên &acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xi&#7871;t bao khí ngo&#7841;n kim ng&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#7911; Mai l&#297;nh tr&#432;&#7899;c, ph&#7911; tr&#7847;n 
      l&#297;nh sau.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7841;c, vàng, ngà, ng&#7885;c thi&#7871;u 
      &#273;&acirc;u,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cung h&#432;&#417;ng, m&atilde; n&atilde;o, minh ch&acirc;u, 
      m&#7845;y bàn.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7905; ràng ph&#7849;m ph&#7909;c m&#7899;i ban,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&atilde;ng bào hai b&#7897;, hoa quan b&#7889;n 
      toà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&ocirc;m sau v&#7915;a r&#7841;ng m&#7891;ng ba,
      </span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cao cao l&#7847;u ph&#432;&#7907;ng, xa xa m&#7863;t 
      r&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ngai vàng th&#259;m th&#7859;m bên trong,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bách quan v&#259;n v&otilde; h&#7897;i &#273;&#7891;ng b&#7845;y

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên tr&#432;&#7899;c, B&#7843;ng nh&atilde;n

      <font color="#111111" size="4">

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ban cho ng&#7921; t&#7917;u, cung hoa,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai yên ng&#7921;a tr&#432;&#7899;c, b&#7889;n toà

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai bên ti&#7871;p &#273;&atilde;i t&#432;ng b&#7915;ng,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#7841;ng nguyên ng&#7921;a tr&#432;&#7899;c song

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&#7891;i ra y&#7871;n &#7849;m m&#7845;y ngày,</span></font></p>

      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phong l&#432;u, phú qu&yacute; cùng chung,</span></font></p>

      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai tài t&#7917;, b&#7889;n thuy&#7873;n quyên,</span></font></p>

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ti&#7871;ng ng&#432;&#7901;i m&#7899;i, v&#7851;n ng&#432;&#7901;i

      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;àn tr&#432;&#7899;c gió, r&#432;&#7907;u bên hoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&acirc;u th&#417; trong tuy&#7871;t, cu&#7897;c c&#7901; 
      d&#432;&#7899;i tr&#259;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">X&#432;&#7899;ng tu&#7923; &#273;&#7873;u gi&#7919; &#273;&#7841;o h&#7857;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t nhà h&atilde;o h&#7907;p d&#7877; ch&#259;ng m&#7845;y 
      ng&#432;&#7901;i!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Có phen &ocirc;n chuy&#7879;n Trùng-&#273;ài,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Có phen nh&#7855;c chuy&#7879;n thoa r&#417;i 
      n&#7921;c c&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Có phen nh&#7855;c chuy&#7879;n l&ocirc;i th&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7871;n Chiêu Qu&acirc;n &#273;i&#7879;n thu&#7903; &#273;&#7901;i 
      Hán gia.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bèn &#273;em t&acirc;m s&#7921; t&acirc;u qua,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i xin &#7903; &#273;&#7845;y &#273;&#7907;i ch&#7901; chiêm 
      bao.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Canh khuya th&#7845;y s&#7913; &#273;òi vào,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7843;o qua m&#7845;y ti&#7871;ng tiêu hao s&#7921; 
      nhà.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&ocirc;m sau &#273;&#7871;n H&#7855;c-thu&#7927;-hà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gieo ch&acirc;u, b&#7895;ng th&#7845;y th&#7847;n &#273;&#432;a 
      &#273;em v&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7857;ng v&acirc;n giá v&#361;, c&#361;ng ghê,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7897;t gi&acirc;y b&#7895;ng th&#7845;y &#273;&#432;a v&#7873; 
      Trung-hoa.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xin làm ng&#7885;c xuy&#7871;n, kim hoa,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#432;&#7907;n ng&#432;&#7901;i th&#432;&#417;ng khách &#273;em 
      &#273;&#432;a sang H&#7891;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7887;i th&#259;m &#273;&#7871;n t&#7899;i linh t&#7915;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Chiêu Qu&acirc;n là hi&#7879;u, b&#7845;y gi&#7901; 
      ti&#7871;n lên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kh&#7845;n r&#7857;ng là c&#7911;a H&#7841;nh Nguyên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cho sai &#273;&#432;a &#273;&#7871;n án ti&#7873;n t&#7841; &acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">L&#7841;i ra các b&#7897; s&#7913; th&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">T&#7841; t&#7915; có thi&#7871;p, v&acirc;n v&acirc;n m&#7845;y 
      l&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Có phen k&#7875; tích Giang Kh&ocirc;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Có phen nh&#7899; n&#7895;i v&#7899;t chài c&#7847;u 
      vui.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hai nhà phúc h&#7853;u v&#7865;n m&#432;&#7901;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Thung th&#259;ng c&otilde;i th&#7885;, &#273;&#7913;ng ng&#7891;i 
      &#273;&#7873;n xu&acirc;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&ocirc;ng hàng l&ocirc;ng ph&#432;&#7907;ng gót l&acirc;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Kìa nhà ng&#361; qu&#7871;, n&#7885; s&acirc;n bát 
      &#273;&#7891;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&#7901;i &#273;&#7901;i khoa m&#7909;c &#273;&#7881;nh chung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&otilde; n&#7873;n thi l&#7877;, n&#7889;i dòng th&#432; 
      h&#432;&#417;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hi&#7871;u, trung, ti&#7871;t, ngh&#297;a &#273;&#7911; 
      &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7921;ng bia mu&ocirc;n ki&#7871;p, &#273;&#7875; g&#432;&#417;ng 
      nghìn &#273;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&nbsp;*</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Cho hay tr&#7901;i c&#361;ng ng&#7891;i r&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tu&#7847;n hoàn &#273;em l&#7841;i vòng dài rút 
      ch&#417;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7919;, lành b&#7887; l&#7917;ng mà coi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">S&#7855;p &#273;&acirc;u vào &#273;&#7845;y, ch&#7859;ng sai 
      ph&acirc;n nào!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Gian tà ch&#7899; v&#7897;i b&#7843;nh bao,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tung lên cho b&#7893;ng, bu&#7897;c vào 
      t&#7921; nhiên.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c dù l&#7887;ng l&#7867;o d&#432;&#7901;ng thiên,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sau càng ráo ri&#7871;t, m&#7899;i tin tay 
      già.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ng&#432;&#7901;i trung còn b&#7887; l&#7917;ng l&#417;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Xoay tr&#259;m nghìn c&#7845;p, th&#7917; ba 
      b&#7889;n l&#7847;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&atilde;y cho &#273;eo &#273;&#7859;ng &#273;&#7911; ph&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">R&otilde; lòng s&#7855;t &#273;á li&#7879;u d&#7847;n g&#7905; 
      cho.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vàng son thêm nét &#273;i&#7875;m tô,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">B&#7845;y gi&#7901; m&#7899;i r&otilde; th&#7921;c lò Hoá 
      c&ocirc;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Truy&#7879;n này xem th&#7845;u thu&#7927; 
      chung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Suy ra m&#7899;i bi&#7871;t tr&#7901;i kh&ocirc;ng có 
      l&#7847;m.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7919;, lành trong m&#7897;t ch&#7919; t&acirc;m,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">C&#417; th&acirc;m thì ho&#7841; c&#361;ng th&acirc;m là 
      th&#432;&#7901;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ví hay tu t&#7881;nh m&#7885;i &#273;&#432;&#7901;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c dù ch&#7883;u thi&#7879;t, sau càng 
      th&#7845;y h&#417;n.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bàn riêng m&#7845;y b&#7841;n kim lan,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Ph&#432;&#7901;ng ta b&#7845;t quá sàn sàn b&#7853;c 
      trung.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">D&#7903; &#273;&acirc;u nh&#432; K&#7927; nh&#432; Tung,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Hay &#273;&acirc;u ví v&#7899;i Mai c&ocirc;ng mà 
      r&#7857;ng.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bình c&#432; gi&#7919; l&#7845;y &#273;&#7841;o h&#7857;ng,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7877; ng&#432;&#7901;i trung chính xin &#273;&#7915;ng 
      t&#7921; khi.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">&#272;&atilde; là nam t&#7917; tu mi,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Vào vòng c&ocirc;ng nghi&#7879;p, &#7845;y thì 
      có vua.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">M&#7899;i hay t&#7915; tóc &#273;&#7871;n t&#417;,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Mi&#7871;ng c&#417;m, t&#7845;m áo, c&#361;ng nh&#7901; 
      qu&acirc;n &acirc;n</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sao cho &#273;&#432;&#7907;c ch&#7919; trung th&#7847;n,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7901; vua ch&#7899; có v&#7883; th&acirc;n m&#7899;i 
      &#273;ành.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&#7871; th&#7901;i tr&#7901;i c&#361;ng có mình,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Lo gì phúc tr&#7841;ch, c&ocirc;ng danh 
      kém ng&#432;&#7901;i.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Phòng v&#259;n nh&acirc;n bu&#7893;i ng&#7891;i r&#7891;i,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">H&#7885;c &#273;òi t&#7845;p t&#7875;nh m&#7845;y l&#7901;i n&ocirc;m 
      na.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Tr&#432;&#7899;c là r&otilde; k&#7867; trung, tà,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Sau là ta d&#7863;n l&#7845;y ta m&#7885;i b&#7873;.</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Bi&#7871;t bao l&#7901;i k&#7879;ch ti&#7871;ng quê,</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font color="#111111" size="4">
      <span style="font-family: Times New Roman">Th&ocirc;i th&ocirc;i b&#7845;t quá là ngh&#7873; mua 
      vui!</span></font></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style="margin-top: 3px; margin-bottom: 3px" align="center">
      <font size="4" color="#111111"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman">
      * H&#7870;T *</span></font></p>
